   111→Feature 3: Unified Inference Gateway with Ollama Support
   112→Architecture: Provider-Agnostic API Layer
   113→The user wants a unified backend API where the application always talks to the same endpoints (/files, /responses), and a routing layer maps logical model names to provider-specific adapters. This is more than a setup script — it's an inference gateway.
   114→App Code  ──>  Gateway API  ──>  Router  ──>  RemoteAdapter (Gemini/Vertex)
   115→                                        ──>  LocalAdapter  (Ollama + PDF extraction)

   145→Gateway API design
   146→pythonclass BaseAdapter(ABC):
   147→    """Provider-agnostic inference adapter."""
   148→
   149→    async def upload_file(self, file_bytes: bytes, filename: str) -> str:
   150→        """Upload file, return file_id. Remote adapters use provider File API.
   151→        Local adapters store to disk and return local file_id."""
   152→
   153→    async def generate(self, file_id: str | None, input_text: str,
   154→                       system_prompt: str) -> str:
   155→        """Generate text response. For remote: passes provider file_id in request.
   156→        For local: retrieves stored file, extracts text, feeds to LLM."""
   157→
   158→    async def generate_structured(self, file_id: str | None, input_text: str,
   159→                                   system_prompt: str,
   160→                                   output_schema: type[BaseModel]) -> BaseModel:
   161→        """Generate structured output. Remote: native structured output.
   162→        Local: JSON mode + Pydantic validation."""
   163→
   164→
   165→class ModelRouter:
   166→    """Routes logical model names to adapters based on config."""
   167→
   168→    def __init__(self, config: dict[str, AdapterConfig]):
   169→        # e.g. {"extraction": GeminiAdapter(...), "grounding": OllamaAdapter(...)}
   170→
   171→    def get_adapter(self, role: str) -> BaseAdapter: ...
   172→
   173→
   174→class InferenceGateway:
   175→    """Unified entry point. App code uses this, never provider SDKs directly."""
   176→
   177→    def __init__(self, router: ModelRouter, file_store: FileStore): ...
   178→
   179→    async def upload_file(self, file_bytes: bytes, filename: str) -> str:
   180→        """Store file in file_store, return gateway file_id."""
   181→
   182→    async def generate(self, role: str, file_id: str | None,
   183→                       input_text: str, system_prompt: str) -> str:
   184→        """Route to appropriate adapter by role."""
   185→
   186→    async def generate_structured(self, role: str, ...) -> BaseModel:
   187→        """Route to appropriate adapter for structured output."""
   188→```
   189→
   190→### Local adapter flow (Ollama)
   191→
   192→1. `upload_file()` → Save PDF bytes to local temp storage, return `file_id` (UUID)
   193→2. `generate()` with `file_id` → Retrieve PDF bytes → Extract text via `pymupdf4llm.to_markdown()` → Send text + system prompt to `ollama.chat()` → Return response
   194→3. `generate_structured()` → Same as above but request `format: "json"` from Ollama and validate with Pydantic schema
   195→
   196→### Remote adapter flow (Gemini)
   197→
   198→1. `upload_file()` → Upload to Gemini File API via `client.files.upload()`, store mapping `gateway_file_id → gemini_file_name`
   199→2. `generate()` with `file_id` → Look up `gemini_file_name` → Call `client.models.generate_content(contents=[gemini_file, prompt])`
   200→3. `generate_structured()` → Same with `response_schema=output_schema`
   201→
   202→### Setup script flow (`scripts/setup-local-models.sh`)
   203→```
   204→1. Detect hardware (NVIDIA GPU / Apple Silicon / CPU)
   205→2. Check if Ollama is installed
   206→3. Install Ollama if needed (brew install ollama / curl)
   207→4. Start ollama serve (background)
   208→5. Pull models:
   209→   - For grounding: attempt medgemma conversion, fall back to gemma2:9b
   210→   - For extraction: pull qwen2.5:14b or llama3.1:8b (capable of JSON mode)
   211→6. Write configuration to .env.local:
   212→   MODEL_BACKEND=ollama
   213→   OLLAMA_BASE_URL=http://localhost:11434
   214→   OLLAMA_MODEL=gemma2:9b
   215→   OLLAMA_EXTRACTION_MODEL=qwen2.5:14b
   216→   LOCAL_EXTRACTION_ENABLED=true
   217→7. Run validation script
   218→Dependencies to add
   219→
   220→langchain-community>=0.3.0 — For ChatOllama LangChain adapter
   221→langchain-openai>=0.2.0 — For OpenAI-compatible vLLM adapter (future)
   222→pymupdf4llm>=0.2.9 — Already present, used for local PDF text extraction
   223→
   224→MedGemma availability note
   225→MedGemma 4B-IT is a gated HuggingFace model not available in Ollama's library. The script will:
   226→
   227→Attempt GGUF conversion if user has HF access (llama.cpp convert_hf_to_gguf.py)
   228→Fall back to gemma2:9b (Google's open model, good medical knowledge) as default
   229→Document how to add MedGemma once GGUF becomes available

---

## THOROUGH CODEBASE EXPLORATION REPORT

I have completed a comprehensive exploration of the medgemma-hackathon codebase. Here's the complete structure and key findings:

### 1. OVERALL DIRECTORY STRUCTURE

**Top Level:**
- `/apps/hitl-ui` - React frontend for Human-in-the-Loop review system
- `/libs` - Shared Python and TypeScript libraries
- `/services` - Microservices (API and protocol processor)
- `/tests` - E2E test suite
- `pyproject.toml` - Root Python workspace config
- `Makefile`, `README.md` - Build and documentation

**Libs (Shared Components):**
- `libs/inference` - MedGemma model loading (Vertex AI and local GPU)
- `libs/shared` - Python utilities (OMOP client, caching, resilience)
- `libs/shared-ts` - TypeScript type definitions
- `libs/events-py` - Event outbox pattern implementation
- `libs/events-ts` - TypeScript event types
- `libs/data-pipeline` - Data loading and preprocessing
- `libs/evaluation` - Evaluation utilities
- `libs/model-training` - Model training infrastructure

**Services:**
- `services/api-service` - FastAPI backend for UI and protocol management
- `services/protocol-processor-service` - LangGraph-based extraction and grounding pipeline

---

### 2. LIBS/INFERENCE/SRC/INFERENCE/ - KEY FILES

**config.py** (75 lines):
- `AgentConfig` dataclass with two backend options: "local" (GPU) and "vertex" (Vertex AI)
- Environment variable configuration: `MODEL_BACKEND`, `MEDGEMMA_MODEL_PATH`, `MEDGEMMA_QUANTIZATION`, `MEDGEMMA_MAX_TOKENS`
- `supports_tools` property checks if backend supports native tool calling
- `from_env()` classmethod creates config from environment

**model_garden.py** (547 lines):
- Two chat model implementations:
  1. `ModelGardenChatModel` - Wraps Vertex AI endpoints with Gemma chat template formatting and exponential-backoff retry
  2. `LocalMedGemmaChatModel` - Loads models from HuggingFace with 4-bit/8-bit quantization via bitsandbytes
- Both implement LangChain `BaseChatModel` interface
- `_build_gemma_prompt()` - Formats messages using Gemma chat template with `<start_of_turn>` markers
- `_is_retryable_error()` - Distinguishes transient (retry) from permanent (no-retry) errors
- `_predict_with_retry()` - Vertex AI endpoint calls with retry decorator
- `create_model_loader()` - Factory returns lazy singleton loader based on config
- Requires: `torch`, `transformers`, `accelerate`, `bitsandbytes` (local) or `google-cloud-aiplatform`, `vertexai`, `langchain-google-genai` (vertex)

---

### 3. SERVICES/PROTOCOL-PROCESSOR-SERVICE/SRC/PROTOCOL_PROCESSOR/TOOLS/ - KEY FILES

**gemini_extractor.py** (210 lines):
- `extract_criteria_structured()` - Main async function
- Uploads PDF to Gemini File API, calls Gemini with structured output using `ExtractionResult` schema
- Returns JSON string (not dict) to minimize LangGraph state size
- Uses Jinja2 templates: `system.jinja2`, `user.jinja2` from prompts directory
- Renders templates with `protocol_title` variable via `render_prompts()` utility
- Decorated with `@gemini_breaker` (circuit breaker) and `@retry` (3 attempts)
- Handles validation errors with `_format_validation_error()` for safe logging
- Cleans up: temp PDF file and uploaded file from File API in finally block

**medgemma_decider.py** (422 lines):
- Two-model architecture: MedGemma for medical reasoning, Gemini for structured output
- `medgemma_decide()` - Main function, selects best terminology match for an entity
  - Takes entity dict, list of `GroundingCandidate` objects, criterion_context
  - Returns `EntityGroundingResult` with selected code and confidence
  - Uses templates: `grounding_system.jinja2`, `grounding_evaluate.jinja2`
  - Calls MedGemma via `_get_medgemma_model()` (lazy singleton from inference module)
  - Structures MedGemma output using Gemini `with_structured_output(GroundingDecision)`
  - Returns confidence 0.0 if no candidates available
- `agentic_reasoning_loop()` - 3-question reasoning for failed grounding retry
  - Q1: Is entity a valid medical criterion? (should_skip)
  - Q2: Does entity map to a standard concept? (is_derived, derived_term)
  - Q3: Can entity be rephrased? (rephrased_query)
  - Uses templates: `grounding_reasoning.jinja2` for prompts
  - Returns `AgenticReasoningResult` with structured 3-question answers
  - Gemini can also add `gemini_suggestion` for collaborative reformulation
- `GroundingDecision` Pydantic model: selected_code, selected_system, preferred_term, confidence (0.0-1.0), reasoning
- `AgenticReasoningResult` Pydantic model: should_skip, is_derived, derived_term, rephrased_query, gemini_suggestion, reasoning

**terminology_router.py** (231 lines):
- Routes entities to terminology APIs via ToolUniverse SDK based on YAML config
- `TerminologyRouter` class:
  - Loads entity type → API mapping from `config/routing.yaml`
  - `get_apis_for_entity()` - Returns list of API names or empty list for skipped types
  - `route_entity()` - Async method, queries all matching APIs, returns list of `GroundingCandidate` objects
  - `_query_tooluniverse()` - Calls `search_terminology()` from `tooluniverse_client.py`, max 10 results
  - Error accumulation: individual API failures logged but not propagated
- Retry logic: transient errors (429, 408, 5xx) retried with exponential backoff
- Caching: optional diskcache with 7-day TTL in user cache directory
- Explicit skip handling: Consent entities with `skip: true` in config not grounded
- Demographic entities routed normally; MedGemma handles derived entity mapping

**gemini_utils.py** (71 lines):
- `create_structured_llm()` - Creates Gemini client with structured output for Pydantic schema
  - Guards on `GOOGLE_API_KEY` environment variable
  - Uses late import of `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI` to avoid import errors
  - Uses `gemini_model_name` from env or defaults to "gemini-2.5-flash"
  - Returns None if key not set or client creation fails
- `parse_structured_output()` - Handles dict or model instance returned by LangChain's `with_structured_output()`

**field_mapper.py** (194 lines):
- Generates suggested field mappings for grounded entities during grounding phase
- `generate_field_mappings()` - Async, decomposes criterion into AutoCriteria field mappings
  - Takes `EntityGroundingResult` and criterion_text
  - Uses Gemini structured output with `FieldMappingResponse` schema
  - Returns list of dicts: entity, relation, value, entity_code, entity_system, omop_concept_id, entity_type
  - Errors logged and empty list returned (best-effort, not propagated)
- `FieldMappingValue` model: typed value with discriminator (standard/range/temporal)
- `FieldMappingItem` model: entity, relation, value (typed), unit
- Relation mapping normalizes legacy operators (has→contains, is→=, etc.) to frontend-standard set

---

### 4. SERVICES/PROTOCOL-PROCESSOR-SERVICE/SRC/PROTOCOL_PROCESSOR/SCHEMAS/ - KEY FILES

**extraction.py** (199 lines):
- `AssertionStatus` enum: PRESENT, ABSENT, HYPOTHETICAL, HISTORICAL, CONDITIONAL
- `TemporalConstraint` - Optional time-based requirements (duration, relation, reference_point)
- `NumericThreshold` - Lab values, age ranges, dosages with value, unit, comparator (>=, <=, >, <, ==, range), upper_value for ranges
- `ExtractedCriterion` - Single criterion with text, criteria_type (inclusion/exclusion), category, temporal_constraint, conditions[], numeric_thresholds[], assertion_status, confidence, source_section, page_number
- `ExtractionResult` - Top-level: criteria list, protocol_summary
- Max lengths: criterion text 12,000 chars, condition phrase 400 chars, summary 2,000 chars

**grounding.py** (138 lines):
- `GroundingCandidate` - Single API match: source_api, code, preferred_term, semantic_type (UMLS-specific), score (0.0-1.0)
- `EntityGroundingResult` - After MedGemma selection:
  - entity_text, entity_type
  - selected_code, selected_system, preferred_term, confidence
  - candidates[], reasoning
  - field_mappings[] (suggested AutoCriteria decomposition)
  - criterion_id (FK reference), omop_concept_id, reconciliation_status
- `GroundingBatchResult` - Batch results: results[], errors[] (error accumulation pattern)

**structure.py** (111 lines):
- LLM output schemas for logic detection (Gemini structured output):
  - `LogicNode` - tree_type (AND/OR/NOT/ATOMIC), field_mapping_index, children[]
  - `LogicDetectionResponse` - root (LogicNode), reasoning
- Storage schemas for JSONB (Criteria.structured_criterion):
  - `ExpressionNode` - type, atomic_criterion_id, entity, relation, value, unit, omop_concept_id, children[]
  - `StructuredCriterionTree` - root (ExpressionNode), structure_confidence (llm/fallback), structure_model name

---

### 5. APPS/HITL-UI/SRC/ - FRONTEND STRUCTURE

**App.tsx** (122 lines):
- React Router with routes:
  - `/login` → LoginPage
  - `/` → Dashboard
  - `/protocols` → ProtocolList
  - `/protocols/:id` → ProtocolDetail
  - `/reviews` → ReviewQueue
  - `/reviews/:batchId` → ReviewPage
  - `/entities/:batchId` → EntityList
  - `/search` → SearchPage
- `RequireAuth()` component wrapping authenticated routes
- Navigation header with user info and logout button
- Sonner toast notifications

**Directory Structure:**
- `src/components/` - 23 component files
- `src/screens/` - 10+ page components
- `src/hooks/` - 8 custom hooks (useReviews, useApi, useAuth, useProtocols, useEntities, useCorpus, useSearch, useTerminologySearch)
- `src/lib/` - utilities (fetchApi.ts)
- `src/stores/` - Zustand state management

**Key Components:**
- `TerminologyBadge.tsx` (66 lines) - Badge display for terminology systems (rxnorm, icd10, snomed, loinc, hpo, umls) with system-specific colors
- `FieldMappingBadges.tsx` (132 lines) - Displays field mappings with entity/relation/value breakdown, normalizes relations, formats values (standard/range/temporal), supports edit callback
- `StructuredFieldEditor.tsx` - Multi-mapping editor component
- `CriterionCard.tsx` - Card displaying criterion with entities and field mappings
- `EntityCard.tsx` - Card for individual grounded entity

**lib/fetchApi.ts** (78 lines):
- `fetchApi()` - Authenticated fetch with JWT bearer token
  - Handles 401 → session expired
  - Uses `useAuthStore` for token management
  - Throws `SessionExpiredError` on 401
- `fetchPublicApi()` - Unauthenticated fetch for /health, /ready
- `API_BASE_URL` from `VITE_API_URL` env or default localhost:8000

**useReviews.ts** (244 lines):
- TypeScript interfaces:
  - `CriteriaBatch` - id, protocol_id, protocol_title, status (pending_review|in_progress|entities_grounded|reviewed|approved|rejected), criteria_count, reviewed_count
  - `Criterion` - id, batch_id, criteria_type, category, text, temporal_constraint, conditions, numeric_thresholds, assertion_status, confidence, source_section, page_number, entities[], review_status
  - `CriterionEntity` - id, entity_type, text, umls_cui, snomed_code, preferred_term, grounding_confidence
  - `FieldMapping` - entity, entity_code, entity_system, omop_concept_id, relation, value
  - `ReviewActionRequest` - action (approve/reject/modify), reviewer_id, modified_text/type/category, modified_structured_fields, comment, reject_reasons[]
- Query hooks: useBatchList, useBatchesByProtocol, useBatchCriteria, usePdfUrl, useAuditLog, usePendingSummary, usePipelineSummary
- Mutation hook: useReviewAction
- All use React Query (TanStack Query)

**fieldMappingUtils.ts** (325 lines):
- `normalizeFieldValue()` - Converts raw values to typed FieldValue (standard/range/temporal)
- `buildInitialValues()` - Creates form values from criterion data:
  - Priority 1: existing field_mappings
  - Priority 2: infer from entities + numeric_thresholds + temporal_constraint
- `normalizeRelation()` - Maps legacy operators to RelationOperator set
- `extractThresholdsList()` - Handles 3 shapes: {thresholds: []}, [], or single object
- `formatTemporalConstraint()`, `formatNumericThreshold()` - Display formatters

**structured-editor/types.ts** (62 lines):
- `RelationOperator` union: =, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within, not_in_last, contains, not_contains
- `FieldValue` discriminated union: StandardValue|RangeValue|TemporalValue
- `FieldMapping` - entity, entity_code, entity_system, omop_concept_id, relation, value
- `StructuredFieldFormValues` - mappings[]

### 7. PIPELINE STATE AND PATTERNS

**state.py** (60 lines):
- `PipelineState` TypedDict for 7-node pipeline: ingest → extract → parse → ground → persist → structure → ordinal_resolve
- Minimal flat state using JSON strings to minimize LangGraph token usage
- Key fields:
  - protocol_id, file_uri, title (always present)
  - batch_id (created by parse)
  - pdf_bytes, extraction_json, entities_json, grounded_entities_json (populated on-demand)
  - archived_reviewed_criteria (for re-extraction)
  - ordinal_proposals_json (from ordinal_resolve)
  - status (processing/completed/failed), error, errors[]

---

### 8. KEY ARCHITECTURAL PATTERNS

**Two-Model Architecture:**
- MedGemma (google/medgemma-4b-it) - Medical domain reasoning
- Gemini - Structured output parsing and JSON formatting
- Both lazy-loaded singletons via inference module

**Error Accumulation:**
- Partial failures preserved alongside successes
- API errors logged but not propagated
- Entity-level failures don't stop batch processing

**Field Mapping Decomposition:**
- AutoCriteria pattern: Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time
- Generated during grounding phase by field_mapper.py
- Editor supports: standard (single value), range (min/max), temporal (duration)
- Frontend normalizes relations: has→contains, is→=, ==→=, range→within, not→not_contains

**Terminology Routing:**
- Config-driven via routing.yaml
- 6 systems: UMLS, SNOMED, ICD-10, LOINC, RxNorm, HPO
- ToolUniverse SDK wrapper with optional caching
- Consent entities explicitly skipped
- Demographic entities routed normally (MedGemma handles derived mapping)

**Resilience:**
- Circuit breakers for Gemini, GCS, Vertex AI
- Exponential backoff retry on transient errors
- Permanent errors (401, 403, 422) not retried

## SUMMARY

This is a comprehensive medical trial eligibility criteria extraction and review system using:

**Backend:**
- FastAPI REST API with SQLModel ORM
- LangGraph 7-node pipeline (extract → ground → structure)
- MedGemma + Gemini two-model architecture
- ToolUniverse for terminology routing (UMLS, SNOMED, ICD-10, LOINC, RxNorm, HPO)
- Circuit breakers and exponential backoff retry

**Frontend:**
- React 18 with Vite
- Zustand state management
- React Query for server state
- Radix UI + Tailwind CSS for UI
- Structured field mapping editor with typed value support

**Data Patterns:**
- Field mapping decomposition: Entity-Relation-Value-Unit-Time (AutoCriteria)
- Temporal constraints and numeric thresholds with assertion status
- Error accumulation for partial success
- OMOP concept ID mapping for downstream CDM export

All files are production-ready with comprehensive error handling, retry logic, and audit logging throughout.

     1→"""Configuration for MedGemma-based agents.
     2→
     3→This module is shared across services to keep env var semantics consistent.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→from __future__ import annotations
     7→
     8→import os
     9→from dataclasses import dataclass
    10→
    11→
    12→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    13→class AgentConfig:
    14→    """Configuration for MedGemma agent infrastructure.
    15→
    16→    Attributes:
    17→        backend: Model backend selection ("local" or "vertex").
    18→        model_path: HuggingFace model ID or local path.
    19→        quantization: Quantization level ("4bit", "8bit", or "none").
    20→        max_new_tokens: Maximum tokens generated for a single call.
    21→        gcp_project_id: GCP project ID (required for Vertex backend).
    22→        gcp_region: GCP region (required for Vertex backend).
    23→        vertex_endpoint_id: Vertex AI endpoint ID (required for Vertex backend).
    24→        vertex_model_name: Vertex model name (optional alternative to endpoint).
    25→        vertex_endpoint_url: Dedicated endpoint URL (optional, auto-detected).
    26→    """
    27→
    28→    backend: str = "local"
    29→    model_path: str = "google/medgemma-4b-it"
    30→    quantization: str = "4bit"
    31→    max_new_tokens: int = 4096
    32→    gcp_project_id: str = ""
    33→    gcp_region: str = "europe-west4"
    34→    vertex_endpoint_id: str = ""
    35→    vertex_model_name: str = ""
    36→    vertex_endpoint_url: str = ""
    37→
    38→    @property
    39→    def supports_tools(self) -> bool:
    40→        """Check if the configured model backend supports native tool calling."""
    41→        if self.backend != "vertex":
    42→            return False
    43→        return bool(self.vertex_model_name)
    44→
    45→    @classmethod
    46→    def from_env(cls) -> "AgentConfig":
    47→        """Create AgentConfig from environment variables."""
    48→        raw_backend = (
    49→            os.getenv("MODEL_BACKEND") or os.getenv("MEDGEMMA_BACKEND") or "local"
    50→        ).strip()
    51→        backend = raw_backend.lower()
    52→        if backend not in {"local", "vertex"}:
    53→            backend = "local"
    54→
    55→        raw_max_tokens = os.getenv("MEDGEMMA_MAX_TOKENS", "2048")
    56→        try:
    57→            max_tokens = int(raw_max_tokens)
    58→        except ValueError:
    59→            max_tokens = 2048
    60→        if max_tokens <= 0:
    61→            max_tokens = 2048
    62→        return cls(
    63→            backend=backend,
    64→            model_path=os.getenv("MEDGEMMA_MODEL_PATH", cls.model_path),
    65→            quantization=os.getenv("MEDGEMMA_QUANTIZATION", cls.quantization),
    66→            max_new_tokens=max_tokens,
    67→            gcp_project_id=os.getenv("GCP_PROJECT_ID", cls.gcp_project_id),
    68→            gcp_region=os.getenv("GCP_REGION", cls.gcp_region),
    69→            vertex_endpoint_id=os.getenv("VERTEX_ENDPOINT_ID", cls.vertex_endpoint_id),
    70→            vertex_model_name=os.getenv("VERTEX_MODEL_NAME", cls.vertex_model_name),
    71→            vertex_endpoint_url=os.getenv(
    72→                "VERTEX_ENDPOINT_URL", cls.vertex_endpoint_url
    73→            ),
    74→        )

     1→"""MedGemma model loading for Vertex AI and local GPU backends.
     2→
     3→Provides two deployment paths for MedGemma (google/medgemma-4b-it):
     4→
     5→1. **Vertex AI Model Garden** (``MODEL_BACKEND=vertex``):
     6→   Uses ``ModelGardenChatModel``, a LangChain BaseChatModel wrapper that
     7→   calls a Vertex AI endpoint with Gemma chat template formatting and
     8→   exponential-backoff retry for transient errors.
     9→
    10→2. **Local GPU** (``MODEL_BACKEND=local``):
    11→   Uses ``LocalMedGemmaChatModel``, which loads the HuggingFace model
    12→   locally with optional 4-bit/8-bit quantization via bitsandbytes.
    13→   Requires an NVIDIA GPU with 8 GB+ VRAM for the default 4-bit config.
    14→
    15→Both backends expose a standard LangChain ``BaseChatModel`` interface so
    16→the rest of the pipeline (``medgemma_decider.py``, ``ground.py``) is
    17→backend-agnostic.
    18→"""
    19→
    20→from __future__ import annotations
    21→
    22→import logging
    23→import time
    24→from typing import Any, Callable
    25→
    26→import requests  # type: ignore[import-untyped]
    27→from langchain_core.callbacks.manager import CallbackManagerForLLMRun
    28→from langchain_core.language_models.chat_models import BaseChatModel
    29→from langchain_core.messages import AIMessage, BaseMessage
    30→from langchain_core.outputs import ChatGeneration, ChatResult
    31→from pydantic import Field
    32→from shared.lazy_cache import lazy_singleton
    33→from tenacity import (
    34→    retry,
    35→    retry_if_exception,
    36→    stop_after_attempt,
    37→    wait_exponential,
    38→)
    39→
    40→from inference.config import AgentConfig
    41→
    42→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    43→
    44→
    45→def _build_gemma_prompt(messages: list[Any]) -> str:
    46→    """Build Gemma chat template prompt from messages.
    47→
    48→    Args:
    49→        messages: List of LangChain messages.
    50→
    51→    Returns:
    52→        Formatted prompt string.
    53→    """
    54→    prompt_parts = []
    55→    for msg in messages:
    56→        role = "user"
    57→        if msg.type == "ai":
    58→            role = "model"
    59→
    60→        content = msg.content
    61→        if not isinstance(content, str):
    62→            content = str(content)
    63→
    64→        if role == "user":
    65→            content = f"### Instruction:\n{content}"
    66→
    67→        prompt_parts.append(f"<start_of_turn>{role}\n{content}<end_of_turn>")
    68→
    69→    return "\n".join(prompt_parts) + "\n<start_of_turn>model\n"
    70→
    71→
    72→def _strip_model_garden_artifacts(text: str, full_prompt: str) -> str:
    73→    """Strip echoed prompts and end-of-turn markers from response.
    74→
    75→    Note: This is now minimal because Gemini handles JSON structuring.
    76→    MedGemma's output goes to Gemini, so thinking tokens and other artifacts
    77→    are handled by Gemini's structured output parser.
    78→    """
    79→    # Strip echoed prompt if present
    80→    if text.startswith(full_prompt):
    81→        text = text[len(full_prompt) :].strip()
    82→
    83→    # Strip trailing end-of-turn marker
    84→    if text.endswith("<end_of_turn>"):
    85→        text = text[: -len("<end_of_turn>")].strip()
    86→
    87→    return text
    88→
    89→
    90→def _is_retryable_error(exception: BaseException) -> bool:
    91→    """Check if an exception is retryable (transient error).
    92→
    93→    Args:
    94→        exception: The exception to check.
    95→
    96→    Returns:
    97→        True if the exception is retryable, False otherwise.
    98→    """
    99→    # Retry on network/connection errors
   100→    if isinstance(
   101→        exception,
   102→        (
   103→            requests.exceptions.ConnectionError,
   104→            requests.exceptions.Timeout,
   105→            requests.exceptions.ChunkedEncodingError,
   106→        ),
   107→    ):
   108→        return True
   109→
   110→    # Retry on Google API transient server errors
   111→    try:
   112→        from google.api_core import exceptions as google_exceptions
   113→
   114→        if isinstance(
   115→            exception,
   116→            (
   117→                google_exceptions.ServiceUnavailable,
   118→                google_exceptions.InternalServerError,
   119→                google_exceptions.DeadlineExceeded,
   120→                google_exceptions.ResourceExhausted,
   121→            ),
   122→        ):
   123→            return True
   124→
   125→        # Do NOT retry on client errors
   126→        if isinstance(
   127→            exception,
   128→            (
   129→                google_exceptions.PermissionDenied,
   130→                google_exceptions.Unauthenticated,
   131→                google_exceptions.InvalidArgument,
   132→                google_exceptions.NotFound,
   133→                google_exceptions.AlreadyExists,
   134→                google_exceptions.FailedPrecondition,
   135→                google_exceptions.OutOfRange,
   136→            ),
   137→        ):
   138→            return False
   139→    except ImportError:
   140→        pass  # google-api-core not installed; skip Google-specific checks
   141→
   142→    # Do NOT retry on expired credentials — no point retrying with the same creds
   143→    try:
   144→        from google.auth.exceptions import RefreshError
   145→
   146→        if isinstance(exception, RefreshError):
   147→            return False
   148→    except ImportError:
   149→        pass  # google-auth not installed; skip RefreshError check
   150→
   151→    return False
   152→
   153→
   154→@retry(
   155→    retry=retry_if_exception(_is_retryable_error),
   156→    stop=stop_after_attempt(3),
   157→    wait=wait_exponential(multiplier=1, min=1, max=10),
   158→    reraise=True,
   159→)
   160→def _predict_with_retry(
   161→    endpoint: Any, instances: list[dict[str, Any]], parameters: dict[str, Any]
   162→) -> Any:
   163→    """Call Vertex AI endpoint.predict with retry on transient errors.
   164→
   165→    Args:
   166→        endpoint: Vertex AI Endpoint object.
   167→        instances: List of prediction instances.
   168→        parameters: Prediction parameters.
   169→
   170→    Returns:
   171→        Prediction response from the endpoint.
   172→    """
   173→    return endpoint.predict(instances=instances, parameters=parameters)
   174→
   175→
   176→class ModelGardenChatModel(BaseChatModel):
   177→    """LangChain ChatModel wrapper for Vertex AI Model Garden endpoints.
   178→
   179→    Wraps a Vertex AI endpoint that serves MedGemma (or other Gemma-family
   180→    models) using the Gemma chat template for prompt formatting and
   181→    exponential-backoff retry for transient errors.
   182→
   183→    The Endpoint object is instantiated once in the constructor and reused
   184→    across calls to enable gRPC channel reuse and avoid per-call overhead.
   185→    """
   186→
   187→    endpoint_resource_name: str
   188→    project: str
   189→    location: str
   190→    max_output_tokens: int = Field(default=8192)
   191→    _endpoint: Any = None
   192→
   193→    def model_post_init(self, __context: Any) -> None:
   194→        """Initialize the Vertex AI Endpoint once for connection reuse."""
   195→        from google.cloud import aiplatform
   196→
   197→        self._endpoint = aiplatform.Endpoint(self.endpoint_resource_name)
   198→
   199→    def _generate(
   200→        self,
   201→        messages: list[BaseMessage],
   202→        stop: list[str] | None = None,
   203→        run_manager: CallbackManagerForLLMRun | None = None,
   204→        **kwargs: Any,
   205→    ) -> ChatResult:
   206→        """Generate a chat response from the Model Garden endpoint.
   207→
   208→        Args:
   209→            messages: List of LangChain messages.
   210→            stop: Optional stop sequences (unused by Model Garden).
   211→            run_manager: Optional callback manager.
   212→            **kwargs: Additional keyword arguments (e.g. temperature).
   213→
   214→        Returns:
   215→            ChatResult containing the model's response.
   216→        """
   217→        endpoint = self._endpoint
   218→
   219→        full_prompt = _build_gemma_prompt(messages)
   220→
   221→        instance = {
   222→            "prompt": full_prompt,
   223→            "max_tokens": self.max_output_tokens,
   224→            "temperature": kwargs.get("temperature", 0.1),
   225→            "top_p": 0.95,
   226→            "top_k": 40,
   227→        }
   228→        parameters = {
   229→            "max_output_tokens": self.max_output_tokens,
   230→            "temperature": kwargs.get("temperature", 0.1),
   231→        }
   232→
   233→        start_time = time.time()
   234→        try:
   235→            logger.debug(
   236→                "Calling Vertex AI endpoint.predict for endpoint: %s",
   237→                self.endpoint_resource_name,
   238→            )
   239→            response = _predict_with_retry(
   240→                endpoint=endpoint, instances=[instance], parameters=parameters
   241→            )
   242→            duration = time.time() - start_time
   243→            logger.debug(
   244→                "Vertex AI endpoint.predict succeeded in %.2f seconds",
   245→                duration,
   246→            )
   247→        except Exception as e:
   248→            duration = time.time() - start_time
   249→            logger.warning(
   250→                "Vertex AI endpoint.predict failed after %.2f seconds: %s",
   251→                duration,
   252→                e,
   253→                exc_info=True,
   254→            )
   255→            raise
   256→
   257→        text = response.predictions[0]
   258→        text = _strip_model_garden_artifacts(text, full_prompt)
   259→
   260→        message = AIMessage(content=text)
   261→        generation = ChatGeneration(message=message)
   262→        return ChatResult(generations=[generation])
   263→
   264→    @property
   265→    def _llm_type(self) -> str:
   266→        return "vertex_model_garden"
   267→
   268→
   269→def _validate_vertex_config(cfg: AgentConfig) -> tuple[str, str, str, str]:
   270→    """Validate Vertex AI configuration fields.
   271→
   272→    Args:
   273→        cfg: AgentConfig instance.
   274→
   275→    Returns:
   276→        Tuple of (project_id, region, endpoint_id, vertex_model_name).
   277→
   278→    Raises:
   279→        ValueError: If required configuration is missing.
   280→    """
   281→    project_id = (cfg.gcp_project_id or "").strip()
   282→    region = (cfg.gcp_region or "").strip()
   283→    endpoint_id = (cfg.vertex_endpoint_id or "").strip()
   284→    vertex_model_name = (cfg.vertex_model_name or "").strip()
   285→
   286→    if not project_id:
   287→        raise ValueError("GCP_PROJECT_ID is required when MODEL_BACKEND=vertex")
   288→    if not region:
   289→        raise ValueError("GCP_REGION is required when MODEL_BACKEND=vertex")
   290→    if not endpoint_id and not vertex_model_name:
   291→        raise ValueError(
   292→            "VERTEX_ENDPOINT_ID or VERTEX_MODEL_NAME is required when "
   293→            "MODEL_BACKEND=vertex"
   294→        )
   295→
   296→    return project_id, region, endpoint_id, vertex_model_name
   297→
   298→
   299→class LocalMedGemmaChatModel(BaseChatModel):
   300→    """LangChain ChatModel wrapper for locally-loaded MedGemma.
   301→
   302→    Loads google/medgemma-4b-it (or a custom model path) via HuggingFace
   303→    ``transformers`` with optional bitsandbytes quantization. The model
   304→    and tokenizer are loaded once on first call and reused.
   305→
   306→    Requires:
   307→        - NVIDIA GPU with sufficient VRAM (8 GB+ for 4-bit, 16 GB+ for 8-bit)
   308→        - ``torch``, ``transformers``, ``accelerate`` packages
   309→        - ``bitsandbytes`` package (for quantized loading)
   310→    """
   311→
   312→    model_path: str = Field(default="google/medgemma-4b-it")
   313→    quantization: str = Field(default="4bit")
   314→    max_output_tokens: int = Field(default=4096)
   315→    _model: Any = None
   316→    _tokenizer: Any = None
   317→
   318→    def model_post_init(self, __context: Any) -> None:
   319→        """Validate that required packages are available."""
   320→        pass  # Defer actual loading to first call for fast startup
   321→
   322→    def _ensure_loaded(self) -> None:
   323→        """Load model and tokenizer on first use."""
   324→        if self._model is not None:
   325→            return
   326→
   327→        try:
   328→            import torch
   329→            from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
   330→        except ImportError as exc:
   331→            raise ImportError(
   332→                "Local MedGemma backend requires torch, transformers, and "
   333→                "accelerate installed. Install with: "
   334→                "pip install torch transformers accelerate bitsandbytes"
   335→            ) from exc
   336→
   337→        if not torch.cuda.is_available():
   338→            raise RuntimeError(
   339→                "Local MedGemma backend requires an NVIDIA GPU with CUDA. "
   340→                "No CUDA device detected. Use MODEL_BACKEND=vertex for "
   341→                "cloud-based inference instead."
   342→            )
   343→
   344→        logger.info(
   345→            "Loading MedGemma locally: model=%s, quantization=%s",
   346→            self.model_path,
   347→            self.quantization,
   348→        )
   349→
   350→        model_kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
   351→            "device_map": "auto",
   352→            "torch_dtype": torch.bfloat16,
   353→        }
   354→
   355→        if self.quantization in ("4bit", "8bit"):
   356→            try:
   357→                from transformers import BitsAndBytesConfig
   358→            except ImportError as exc:
   359→                raise ImportError(
   360→                    "Quantized loading requires bitsandbytes. "
   361→                    "Install with: pip install bitsandbytes"
   362→                ) from exc
   363→
   364→            if self.quantization == "4bit":
   365→                model_kwargs["quantization_config"] = BitsAndBytesConfig(
   366→                    load_in_4bit=True,
   367→                    bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=torch.bfloat16,
   368→                    bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=True,
   369→                    bnb_4bit_quant_type="nf4",
   370→                )
   371→            else:
   372→                model_kwargs["quantization_config"] = BitsAndBytesConfig(
   373→                    load_in_8bit=True,
   374→                )
   375→
   376→        start_time = time.time()
   377→        self._tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(self.model_path)
   378→        self._model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
   379→            self.model_path, **model_kwargs
   380→        )
   381→        duration = time.time() - start_time
   382→        logger.info("MedGemma loaded in %.1f seconds", duration)
   383→
   384→    def _generate(
   385→        self,
   386→        messages: list[BaseMessage],
   387→        stop: list[str] | None = None,
   388→        run_manager: CallbackManagerForLLMRun | None = None,
   389→        **kwargs: Any,
   390→    ) -> ChatResult:
   391→        """Generate a response using the locally-loaded MedGemma model.
   392→
   393→        Args:
   394→            messages: List of LangChain messages.
   395→            stop: Optional stop sequences.
   396→            run_manager: Optional callback manager.
   397→            **kwargs: Additional keyword arguments (e.g. temperature).
   398→
   399→        Returns:
   400→            ChatResult containing the model's response.
   401→        """
   402→        import torch
   403→
   404→        self._ensure_loaded()
   405→
   406→        full_prompt = _build_gemma_prompt(messages)
   407→
   408→        inputs = self._tokenizer(full_prompt, return_tensors="pt").to(
   409→            self._model.device
   410→        )
   411→        input_length = inputs["input_ids"].shape[1]
   412→
   413→        start_time = time.time()
   414→        with torch.no_grad():
   415→            outputs = self._model.generate(
   416→                **inputs,
   417→                max_new_tokens=self.max_output_tokens,
   418→                temperature=kwargs.get("temperature", 0.1),
   419→                top_p=0.95,
   420→                top_k=40,
   421→                do_sample=True,
   422→            )
   423→        duration = time.time() - start_time
   424→
   425→        # Decode only the generated tokens (exclude prompt)
   426→        generated_tokens = outputs[0][input_length:]
   427→        text = self._tokenizer.decode(generated_tokens, skip_special_tokens=True)
   428→        text = _strip_model_garden_artifacts(text, full_prompt)
   429→
   430→        logger.debug(
   431→            "Local MedGemma generated %d tokens in %.2f seconds",
   432→            len(generated_tokens),
   433→            duration,
   434→        )
   435→
   436→        message = AIMessage(content=text)
   437→        generation = ChatGeneration(message=message)
   438→        return ChatResult(generations=[generation])
   439→
   440→    @property
   441→    def _llm_type(self) -> str:
   442→        return "local_medgemma"
   443→
   444→
   445→def create_model_loader(config: AgentConfig | None = None) -> Callable[[], Any]:
   446→    """Create a lazy MedGemma model loader for the configured backend.
   447→
   448→    Supports two backends:
   449→        - ``vertex``: Vertex AI Model Garden (cloud, recommended for production)
   450→        - ``local``: Local GPU with HuggingFace transformers (requires NVIDIA GPU)
   451→
   452→    Args:
   453→        config: Agent configuration. Defaults to ``AgentConfig.from_env()``.
   454→
   455→    Returns:
   456→        Callable that loads and returns a LangChain chat model when invoked.
   457→
   458→    Raises:
   459→        ValueError: If required configuration is missing for the selected backend.
   460→    """
   461→    cfg = config or AgentConfig.from_env()
   462→
   463→    if cfg.backend == "vertex":
   464→        return _create_vertex_model_loader(cfg)
   465→
   466→    return _create_local_model_loader(cfg)
   467→
   468→
   469→def _create_vertex_model_loader(cfg: AgentConfig) -> Callable[[], Any]:
   470→    """Create a lazy Vertex AI model loader.
   471→
   472→    Args:
   473→        cfg: Validated AgentConfig with backend=="vertex".
   474→
   475→    Returns:
   476→        Callable that lazily initializes and returns the model.
   477→    """
   478→    project_id, region, endpoint_id, vertex_model_name = _validate_vertex_config(cfg)
   479→
   480→    @lazy_singleton
   481→    def load_model() -> Any:
   482→        try:
   483→            import vertexai
   484→        except ImportError as exc:
   485→            raise ImportError(
   486→                "Vertex AI backend requires google-cloud-aiplatform installed."
   487→            ) from exc
   488→
   489→        vertexai.init(project=project_id, location=region)
   490→
   491→        if vertex_model_name:
   492→            try:
   493→                from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
   494→            except ImportError as exc:
   495→                raise ImportError(
   496→                    "Vertex AI backend requires langchain-google-genai installed."
   497→                ) from exc
   498→
   499→            return ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   500→                model=vertex_model_name,
   501→                project=project_id,
   502→                location=region,
   503→                vertexai=True,
   504→                max_output_tokens=cfg.max_new_tokens,
   505→            )
   506→
   507→        endpoint_resource_name = (
   508→            f"projects/{project_id}/locations/{region}/endpoints/{endpoint_id}"
   509→        )
   510→        return ModelGardenChatModel(
   511→            endpoint_resource_name=endpoint_resource_name,
   512→            project=project_id,
   513→            location=region,
   514→            max_output_tokens=cfg.max_new_tokens,
   515→        )
   516→
   517→    return load_model
   518→
   519→
   520→def _create_local_model_loader(cfg: AgentConfig) -> Callable[[], Any]:
   521→    """Create a lazy local MedGemma model loader.
   522→
   523→    Loads MedGemma (default: google/medgemma-4b-it) from HuggingFace
   524→    with optional 4-bit or 8-bit quantization via bitsandbytes.
   525→
   526→    Args:
   527→        cfg: AgentConfig with backend=="local".
   528→
   529→    Returns:
   530→        Callable that lazily initializes and returns the model.
   531→    """
   532→
   533→    @lazy_singleton
   534→    def load_model() -> Any:
   535→        logger.info(
   536→            "Initializing local MedGemma: model=%s, quantization=%s",
   537→            cfg.model_path,
   538→            cfg.quantization,
   539→        )
   540→        return LocalMedGemmaChatModel(
   541→            model_path=cfg.model_path,
   542→            quantization=cfg.quantization,
   543→            max_output_tokens=cfg.max_new_tokens,
   544→        )
   545→
   546→    return load_model
   547→

     1→"""Gemini extractor tool: structured criteria extraction via Gemini File API.
     2→
     3→Uploads PDF to Gemini File API, calls Gemini with structured output using
     4→ExtractionResult as the response schema, and returns a JSON string.
     5→
     6→Returns JSON string (not dict) to minimize LangGraph state size.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import logging
    12→import os
    13→import tempfile
    14→from pathlib import Path
    15→from typing import Any, cast
    16→
    17→from google import genai
    18→from google.genai import types
    19→from inference.factory import render_prompts
    20→from pydantic import ValidationError
    21→from shared.resilience import gemini_breaker
    22→from tenacity import (
    23→    before_sleep_log,
    24→    retry,
    25→    retry_if_exception_type,
    26→    stop_after_attempt,
    27→    wait_random_exponential,
    28→)
    29→
    30→from protocol_processor.schemas.extraction import ExtractionResult
    31→
    32→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    33→
    34→PROMPTS_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "prompts"
    35→
    36→# Max length when formatting validation errors (avoids huge console dumps)
    37→_VALIDATION_ERROR_STR_MAX = 200
    38→
    39→
    40→def _truncate(s: str, max_len: int = _VALIDATION_ERROR_STR_MAX) -> str:
    41→    """Truncate string for safe inclusion in error messages."""
    42→    if len(s) <= max_len:
    43→        return s
    44→    return s[:max_len] + "..."
    45→
    46→
    47→def _format_validation_error(err: ValidationError) -> str:
    48→    """Format ValidationError with truncated input/context for safe logging."""
    49→    errors = err.errors()
    50→    parts = [f"ValidationError ({len(errors)} error(s))"]
    51→    for e in errors:
    52→        loc = ".".join(str(x) for x in e.get("loc", ()))
    53→        msg = e.get("msg", "")
    54→        ctx = e.get("ctx") or {}
    55→        inp = e.get("input")
    56→        part = f"  {loc}: {msg}"
    57→        if ctx:
    58→            part += f" (ctx: {_truncate(str(ctx))})"
    59→        if inp is not None:
    60→            part += f" | input: {_truncate(str(inp))}"
    61→        parts.append(part)
    62→    return "\n".join(parts)
    63→
    64→
    65→@gemini_breaker
    66→@retry(
    67→    retry=retry_if_exception_type(Exception),
    68→    stop=stop_after_attempt(3),
    69→    wait=wait_random_exponential(multiplier=1, min=2, max=10),
    70→    before_sleep=before_sleep_log(logger, logging.WARNING),
    71→    reraise=True,
    72→)
    73→async def _invoke_gemini(
    74→    client: genai.Client,
    75→    model: str,
    76→    uploaded_file: types.File,
    77→    system_prompt: str,
    78→    user_prompt: str,
    79→) -> ExtractionResult:
    80→    """Invoke Gemini with retry and circuit breaker.
    81→
    82→    Args:
    83→        client: Google GenAI client instance.
    84→        model: Model name to use.
    85→        uploaded_file: Uploaded PDF file from File API.
    86→        system_prompt: System instruction.
    87→        user_prompt: User prompt text.
    88→
    89→    Returns:
    90→        ExtractionResult parsed from Gemini's structured output.
    91→    """
    92→    response = await client.aio.models.generate_content(
    93→        model=model,
    94→        contents=cast(Any, [uploaded_file, user_prompt]),
    95→        config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
    96→            system_instruction=system_prompt,
    97→            response_mime_type="application/json",
    98→            response_schema=ExtractionResult,
    99→        ),
   100→    )
   101→
   102→    # Return parsed Pydantic model directly
   103→    if response.parsed is not None:
   104→        return cast(ExtractionResult, response.parsed)
   105→
   106→    # Fallback to parsing response.text if parsed is None
   107→    text = response.text or ""
   108→    return ExtractionResult.model_validate_json(text)
   109→
   110→
   111→async def extract_criteria_structured(
   112→    pdf_bytes: bytes,
   113→    protocol_id: str,
   114→    title: str,
   115→) -> str:
   116→    """Extract criteria from PDF using Gemini File API with structured output.
   117→
   118→    Uploads the PDF to Gemini File API, calls Gemini with ExtractionResult
   119→    as the response schema, and returns the result as a JSON string.
   120→
   121→    Returns JSON string (not dict) to minimize LangGraph state size.
   122→
   123→    Args:
   124→        pdf_bytes: Raw PDF bytes to extract criteria from.
   125→        protocol_id: UUID of the protocol (for logging).
   126→        title: Protocol title (used in user prompt).
   127→
   128→    Returns:
   129→        JSON string representation of ExtractionResult.
   130→
   131→    Raises:
   132→        ValidationError: If Gemini response cannot be parsed as ExtractionResult.
   133→        Exception: On Gemini API or File API errors after retries exhausted.
   134→    """
   135→    tmp_path = None
   136→    uploaded_file = None
   137→    client = None
   138→
   139→    try:
   140→        # Instantiate client
   141→        client = genai.Client(api_key=os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY"))
   142→        model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   143→
   144→        # Write PDF to temp file for File API upload
   145→        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".pdf", delete=False) as tmp:
   146→            tmp.write(pdf_bytes)
   147→            tmp_path = tmp.name
   148→
   149→        # Upload via File API
   150→        uploaded_file = client.files.upload(file=tmp_path)
   151→
   152→        system_prompt, user_prompt = render_prompts(
   153→            prompts_dir=PROMPTS_DIR,
   154→            system_template="system.jinja2",
   155→            user_template="user.jinja2",
   156→            prompt_vars={
   157→                "title": title,
   158→            },
   159→        )
   160→
   161→        extraction_result = await _invoke_gemini(
   162→            client, model_name, uploaded_file, system_prompt, user_prompt
   163→        )
   164→
   165→        logger.info(
   166→            "Extracted %d criteria from protocol %s (Gemini File API)",
   167→            len(extraction_result.criteria),
   168→            protocol_id,
   169→        )
   170→
   171→        # Return as JSON string (not dict) for minimal state
   172→        return extraction_result.model_dump_json()
   173→
   174→    except ValidationError as e:
   175→        msg = _format_validation_error(e)
   176→        logger.error(
   177→            "Extraction validation failed for protocol %s: %s",
   178→            protocol_id,
   179→            msg,
   180→        )
   181→        raise
   182→
   183→    except Exception:
   184→        logger.exception(
   185→            "Extraction failed for protocol %s",
   186→            protocol_id,
   187→        )
   188→        raise
   189→
   190→    finally:
   191→        # Clean up temp file
   192→        if tmp_path:
   193→            try:
   194→                os.unlink(tmp_path)
   195→            except Exception as cleanup_err:
   196→                logger.warning(
   197→                    "Failed to delete temp file %s: %s", tmp_path, cleanup_err
   198→                )
   199→
   200→        # Clean up uploaded file
   201→        if uploaded_file and client and uploaded_file.name:
   202→            try:
   203→                client.files.delete(name=uploaded_file.name)
   204→            except Exception as cleanup_err:
   205→                logger.warning(
   206→                    "Failed to delete uploaded file %s: %s",
   207→                    uploaded_file.name,
   208→                    cleanup_err,
   209→                )
   210→

     1→"""Shared Gemini LLM utilities for protocol processor tools.
     2→
     3→Centralizes the repeated pattern of creating a ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
     4→client with structured output and parsing its response. Used by
     5→structure_builder, ordinal_resolver, and field_mapper.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import logging
    11→import os
    12→from typing import Any, TypeVar
    13→
    14→from pydantic import BaseModel
    15→
    16→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    17→
    18→T = TypeVar("T", bound=BaseModel)
    19→
    20→
    21→def create_structured_llm(
    22→    output_schema: type[T],
    23→) -> Any | None:
    24→    """Create a Gemini LLM client with structured output.
    25→
    26→    Guards on GOOGLE_API_KEY. Uses late import of ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    27→    to avoid import errors when the package is not installed.
    28→
    29→    Args:
    30→        output_schema: Pydantic model class for structured output.
    31→
    32→    Returns:
    33→        A structured LLM instance, or None if GOOGLE_API_KEY is not set
    34→        or client creation fails.
    35→    """
    36→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
    37→    if not google_api_key:
    38→        logger.warning("GOOGLE_API_KEY not set — skipping Gemini call")
    39→        return None
    40→
    41→    try:
    42→        from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    43→
    44→        gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
    45→        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
    46→            model=gemini_model_name,
    47→            google_api_key=google_api_key,
    48→        )
    49→        return gemini.with_structured_output(output_schema)
    50→    except Exception as e:
    51→        logger.warning("Failed to create Gemini client: %s", e)
    52→        return None
    53→
    54→
    55→def parse_structured_output(result: Any, model: type[T]) -> T:
    56→    """Parse a structured LLM output into a Pydantic model.
    57→
    58→    Handles both dict and already-parsed model instances returned
    59→    by LangChain's with_structured_output().
    60→
    61→    Args:
    62→        result: Raw LLM output (dict or model instance).
    63→        model: Pydantic model class to validate against.
    64→
    65→    Returns:
    66→        Validated model instance.
    67→    """
    68→    if isinstance(result, dict):
    69→        return model.model_validate(result)
    70→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
    71→
```

     1→import { Navigate, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
     2→import { Toaster } from 'sonner';
     3→
     4→import { LogOut, User } from 'lucide-react';
     5→import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
     6→import { SessionExpiredModal } from './components/SessionExpiredModal';
     7→import { Button } from './components/ui/Button';
     8→import { useAuth } from './hooks/useAuth';
     9→import Dashboard from './screens/Dashboard';
    10→import EntityList from './screens/EntityList';
    11→import LoginPage from './screens/LoginPage';
    12→import ProtocolDetail from './screens/ProtocolDetail';
    13→import ProtocolList from './screens/ProtocolList';
    14→import ReviewPage from './screens/ReviewPage';
    15→import ReviewQueue from './screens/ReviewQueue';
    16→import SearchPage from './screens/SearchPage';
    17→
    18→function RequireAuth({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
    19→    const { isAuthenticated, isSessionExpired } = useAuth();
    20→    // If session expired, keep showing children behind the modal overlay
    21→    if (!isAuthenticated && !isSessionExpired) {
    22→        return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
    23→    }
    24→    return <>{children}</>;
    25→}
    26→
    27→function App() {
    28→    const { isAuthenticated, user, logout } = useAuth();
    29→
    30→    return (
    31→        <div className="min-h-screen bg-background">
    32→            {/* Navigation header when authenticated */}
    33→            {isAuthenticated && (
    34→                <header className="border-b bg-card">
    35→                    <div className="container mx-auto px-6 py-3 flex items-center justify-between">
    36→                        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
    37→                            <h1 className="text-lg font-semibold">HITL System</h1>
    38→                        </div>
    39→                        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
    40→                            {user && (
    41→                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
    42→                                    <User className="h-4 w-4" />
    43→                                    <span>{user.name || user.email}</span>
    44→                                </div>
    45→                            )}
    46→                            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={logout}>
    47→                                <LogOut className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" />
    48→                                Logout
    49→                            </Button>
    50→                        </div>
    51→                    </div>
    52→                </header>
    53→            )}
    54→
    55→            <Routes>
    56→                <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
    57→                <Route
    58→                    path="/"
    59→                    element={
    60→                        <RequireAuth>
    61→                            <Dashboard />
    62→                        </RequireAuth>
    63→                    }
    64→                />
    65→                <Route
    66→                    path="/protocols"
    67→                    element={
    68→                        <RequireAuth>
    69→                            <ProtocolList />
    70→                        </RequireAuth>
    71→                    }
    72→                />
    73→                <Route
    74→                    path="/protocols/:id"
    75→                    element={
    76→                        <RequireAuth>
    77→                            <ProtocolDetail />
    78→                        </RequireAuth>
    79→                    }
    80→                />
    81→                <Route
    82→                    path="/reviews"
    83→                    element={
    84→                        <RequireAuth>
    85→                            <ReviewQueue />
    86→                        </RequireAuth>
    87→                    }
    88→                />
    89→                <Route
    90→                    path="/reviews/:batchId"
    91→                    element={
    92→                        <RequireAuth>
    93→                            <ReviewPage />
    94→                        </RequireAuth>
    95→                    }
    96→                />
    97→                <Route
    98→                    path="/entities/:batchId"
    99→                    element={
   100→                        <RequireAuth>
   101→                            <EntityList />
   102→                        </RequireAuth>
   103→                    }
   104→                />
   105→                <Route
   106→                    path="/search"
   107→                    element={
   108→                        <RequireAuth>
   109→                            <SearchPage />
   110→                        </RequireAuth>
   111→                    }
   112→                />
   113→            </Routes>
   114→
   115→            <SessionExpiredModal />
   116→            <Toaster richColors position="top-right" />
   117→        </div>
   118→    );
   119→}
   120→
   121→export default App;
   122→

    36→
    37→# Setup basic logging
    38→logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
    39→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    40→
    41→# Track running background tasks
    42→_running_tasks: Set[asyncio.Task] = set()  # noqa: UP006
    43→
    44→
    45→@asynccontextmanager
    46→async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    47→    """Manage the lifecycle of the FastAPI application."""
    48→    # Startup
    49→    logger.info("Starting up API service...")
    50→    logger.info("Initializing database...")
    51→    create_db_and_tables()
    52→    logger.info("Database initialized successfully")
    53→
    54→    # Initialize MLflow
    55→    try:
    56→        import mlflow
    57→
    58→        tracking_uri = os.getenv("MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI")
    59→        if tracking_uri:
    60→            mlflow.set_tracking_uri(tracking_uri)
    61→            mlflow.set_experiment("protocol-processing")
    62→            # Enable LangChain autolog for extraction/grounding agent traces
    63→            try:
    64→                mlflow.langchain.autolog(run_tracer_inline=True)
    65→                logging.getLogger("mlflow.utils.autologging_utils").setLevel(
    66→                    logging.ERROR
    67→                )
    68→                logger.info("MLflow LangChain autolog enabled (run_tracer_inline=True)")
    69→            except Exception:
    70→                logger.debug("MLflow LangChain autolog failed", exc_info=True)
    71→            logger.info(
    72→                "MLflow initialized: tracking_uri=%s, experiment=protocol-processing",
    73→                tracking_uri,
    74→            )
    75→        else:
    76→            logger.info("MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI not set, skipping MLflow initialization")
    77→    except ImportError:
    78→        logger.info("mlflow not installed, skipping initialization")
    79→    except Exception:
    80→        logger.warning(
    81→            "MLflow initialization failed, continuing without tracing",
    82→            exc_info=True,
    83→        )
    84→
    85→    # Start outbox processor as background task (skip in tests — the poll loop
    86→    # and executor shutdown add seconds of overhead per TestClient instantiation).
    87→    processor = None
    88→    if not os.environ.get("TESTING"):
    89→        # Per PIPE-03: criteria_extracted outbox removed. protocol_uploaded retained.
    90→        # protocol_processor.trigger.handle_protocol_uploaded replaces both the
    91→        # old extraction_service.trigger and grounding_service.trigger handlers.
    92→        processor = OutboxProcessor(
    93→            engine=engine,
    94→            handlers={
    95→                "protocol_uploaded": [handle_protocol_uploaded],
    96→            },
    97→        )
    98→        task = asyncio.create_task(processor.start())
    99→        _running_tasks.add(task)
   100→        task.add_done_callback(_running_tasks.discard)
   101→
   102→    yield
   103→
   104→    # Shutdown - stop outbox processor first
   105→    if processor:
   106→        await processor.stop()
   107→
   108→    # Wait for background tasks to complete
   109→    if _running_tasks:
   110→        logger.info(f"Waiting for {len(_running_tasks)} tasks to complete...")
   111→        await asyncio.gather(*_running_tasks, return_exceptions=True)
   112→
   113→    engine.dispose()
   114→    logger.info("Shutdown complete")
   115→
   116→
   117→app = FastAPI(lifespan=lifespan)
   118→
   119→# Add SessionMiddleware for OAuth state (must be before CORS)
   120→_DEFAULT_SESSION_SECRET=[REDACTED]"
   121→_session_secret = os.getenv("SESSION_SECRET", _DEFAULT_SESSION_SECRET)
   122→_is_production = os.getenv("ENVIRONMENT", "").lower() == "production"
   123→if _is_production and _session_secret == _DEFAULT_SESSION_SECRET:
   124→    raise RuntimeError(
   125→        "SESSION_SECRET must be set to a secure value in production. "

   128→app.add_middleware(

   133→# Configure CORS

   135→cors_origins = os.getenv(

   137→    "http://localhost:3000,http://localhost:3001,http://localhost:3002,http://localhost:3003,http://localhost:5173,http://localhost:5174",
   138→).split(",")
   139→
   140→app.add_middleware(

   152→@app.exception_handler(Exception)
   153→async def global_exception_handler(request: Request, exc: Exception) -> JSONResponse:
   154→    """Catch unhandled exceptions and return JSON so CORS headers are preserved."""
   155→    logger.error(
   156→        "Unhandled exception on %s %s: %s", request.method, request.url.path, exc
   157→    )
   158→    return JSONResponse(

   179→def _check_omop_vocab() -> dict:
   180→    """Check OMOP vocab DB connectivity and that tables are loaded.
   181→
   182→    Returns dict with 'status' ('ok' | 'unavailable' | 'not_loaded')
   183→    and optional 'concept_count'.
   184→    """
   185→    omop_url = os.getenv("OMOP_VOCAB_URL")
   186→    if not omop_url:
   187→        return {"status": "unavailable", "error": "OMOP_VOCAB_URL not set"}
   188→    try:
   189→        omop_engine = sa_create_engine(omop_url, pool_pre_ping=True)
   190→        with omop_engine.connect() as conn:
   191→            row = conn.execute(text("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM concept")).scalar()
   192→            if not row or row == 0:
   193→                return {"status": "not_loaded", "concept_count": 0}
   194→            return {"status": "ok", "concept_count": row}
   195→    except Exception as e:
   196→        return {"status": "unavailable", "error": str(e)}
   197→
   198→
   199→def _check_gcs() -> str:
   200→    """Check GCS connectivity and credential validity.
   201→
   202→    Returns one of: "ok" | "auth_expired" | "unavailable" | "local_storage".
   203→    """
   204→    from api_service.gcs import _use_local_storage, get_bucket_name, get_gcs_client
   205→
   206→    if _use_local_storage():
   207→        return "local_storage"
   208→    try:
   209→        client = get_gcs_client()
   210→        if client is None:
   211→            return "unavailable"
   212→        bucket_name = get_bucket_name()
   213→        client.bucket(bucket_name).exists()
   214→        return "ok"
   215→    except Exception as e:
   216→        try:
   217→            from google.auth.exceptions import RefreshError
   218→
   219→            if isinstance(e, RefreshError):
   220→                return "auth_expired"
   221→        except ImportError:
   222→            pass  # google-auth not installed; skip RefreshError detection
   223→        return "unavailable"
   224→
   225→
   226→def _check_breakers() -> dict:
   227→    """Return circuit breaker states as a dict."""
   228→    from shared.resilience import gcs_breaker, gemini_breaker, vertex_ai_breaker
   229→
   230→    return {
   231→        "gemini": str(gemini_breaker.current_state),
   232→        "gcs": str(gcs_breaker.current_state),
   233→        "vertex_ai": str(vertex_ai_breaker.current_state),
   234→    }
   235→
   236→
   237→@app.get("/health")
   238→async def health_check(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
   239→    """Health check — main DB required, OMOP/GCS/breakers informational."""
   240→    checks: dict = {}
   241→
   242→    # Main database (required)
   243→    try:
   244→        db.execute(text("SELECT 1"))
   245→        checks["database"] = "connected"
   246→    except Exception:
   247→        checks["database"] = "unavailable"
   248→        return JSONResponse(
   249→            status_code=503,
   250→            content={"status": "not_ready", "checks": checks},
   251→        )
   252→
   253→    # OMOP vocabulary database (informational — doesn't block health)
   254→    omop = _check_omop_vocab()
   255→    checks["omop_vocab"] = omop["status"]
   256→    if omop.get("concept_count"):
   257→        checks["omop_concept_count"] = omop["concept_count"]
   258→
   259→    # GCS / Google API credentials (informational)
   260→    checks["gcs"] = _check_gcs()
   261→
   262→    # Circuit breaker states (informational)
   263→    checks["breakers"] = _check_breakers()
   264→
   265→    return {"status": "healthy", "checks": checks}
   266→
   267→
   268→@app.get("/ready")
   269→async def readiness_check(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
   270→    """Readiness probe — all dependencies including OMOP must be available."""
   271→    checks: dict = {}
   272→
   273→    try:
   274→        db.execute(text("SELECT 1"))
   275→        checks["database"] = "connected"
   276→    except Exception:
   277→        checks["database"] = "unavailable"
   278→
   279→    omop = _check_omop_vocab()
   280→    checks["omop_vocab"] = omop["status"]
   281→    if omop.get("concept_count"):
   282→        checks["omop_concept_count"] = omop["concept_count"]
   283→
   284→    all_ok = checks["database"] == "connected" and omop["status"] == "ok"
   285→    if not all_ok:
   286→        return JSONResponse(
   287→            status_code=503,
   288→            content={"status": "not_ready", "checks": checks},
   289→        )
   290→    return {"status": "ready", "checks": checks}
   291→
   292→
   293→@app.get("/")
   294→async def root():
   295→    """Returns a welcome message for the API."""
   296→    return {"message": "Welcome to the API Service"}
   297→
   298→
   299→# --- Local file storage endpoints (dev only) ---
   300→
   301→
   302→@app.put("/local-upload/{blob_path:path}")
   303→async def local_upload(blob_path: str, request: Request):
   304→    """Receive a file upload and store it locally (dev mode)."""
   305→    from api_service.gcs import local_save_file
   306→
   307→    body = await request.body()
   308→    try:
   309→        local_save_file(blob_path, body)
   310→    except ValueError:
   311→        return JSONResponse(status_code=400, content={"detail": "Invalid file path"})
   312→    return JSONResponse(status_code=200, content={"status": "ok"})
   313→
   314→
   315→@app.get("/local-files/{blob_path:path}")
   316→async def local_files(blob_path: str):
   317→    """Serve a locally stored file (dev mode)."""
   318→    from api_service.gcs import local_get_file_path
   319→
   320→    try:
   321→        file_path = local_get_file_path(blob_path)
   322→    except ValueError:
   323→        return JSONResponse(status_code=400, content={"detail": "Invalid file path"})
   324→    if file_path is None:
   325→        return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"detail": "File not found"})
   326→    return FileResponse(str(file_path), media_type="application/pdf")
   327→
```

     1→import { toast } from 'sonner';
     2→
     3→import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/authStore';
     4→
     5→export const API_BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || 'http://localhost:8000';
     6→
     7→export class SessionExpiredError extends Error {
     8→    constructor() {
     9→        super('Session expired');
    10→        this.name = 'SessionExpiredError';
    11→    }
    12→}
    13→
    14→let _sessionExpiredToastShown = false;
    15→
    16→function _handleSessionExpired(): void {
    17→    useAuthStore.getState().setSessionExpired();
    18→    if (!_sessionExpiredToastShown) {
    19→        _sessionExpiredToastShown = true;
    20→        toast.error('Session expired — please sign in again', {
    21→            duration: 6000,
    22→            onDismiss: () => {
    23→                _sessionExpiredToastShown = false;
    24→            },
    25→            onAutoClose: () => {
    26→                _sessionExpiredToastShown = false;
    27→            },
    28→        });
    29→    }
    30→}
    31→
    32→/**
    33→ * Authenticated fetch wrapper. Attaches JWT and handles 401 → session expired.
    34→ */
    35→export async function fetchApi<T>(endpoint: string, options?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
    36→    const token = useAuthStore.getState().token;
    37→    const headers: HeadersInit = {
    38→        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    39→        ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
    40→        ...(options?.headers || {}),
    41→    };
    42→
    43→    const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${endpoint}`, {
    44→        ...options,
    45→        headers,
    46→    });
    47→
    48→    if (response.status === 401) {
    49→        _handleSessionExpired();
    50→        throw new SessionExpiredError();
    51→    }
    52→
    53→    if (!response.ok) {
    54→        throw new Error(`API Error: ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`);
    55→    }
    56→
    57→    return response.json();
    58→}
    59→
    60→/**
    61→ * Public (unauthenticated) fetch wrapper for endpoints like /health and /ready.
    62→ */
    63→export async function fetchPublicApi<T>(endpoint: string, options?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
    64→    const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${endpoint}`, {
    65→        ...options,
    66→        headers: {
    67→            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    68→            ...options?.headers,
    69→        },
    70→    });
    71→
    72→    if (!response.ok) {
    73→        throw new Error(`API Error: ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`);
    74→    }
    75→
    76→    return response.json();
    77→}
    78→

   128→// --- Hooks ---
   129→
   130→export function useBatchList(page: number, pageSize: number, status?: string) {
   131→    const params = new URLSearchParams({
   132→        page: String(page),
   133→        page_size: String(pageSize),
   134→    });
   135→    if (status) {
   136→        params.set('status', status);
   137→    }
   138→
   139→    return useQuery({
   140→        queryKey: ['review-batches', page, pageSize, status],
   141→        queryFn: () => fetchApi<BatchListResponse>(`/reviews/batches?${params.toString()}`),
   142→    });
   143→}
   144→
   145→export function useBatchesByProtocol(protocolId: string) {
   146→    return useQuery({
   147→        queryKey: ['review-batches', 'protocol', protocolId],
   148→        queryFn: () =>
   149→            fetchApi<BatchListResponse>(
   150→                `/reviews/batches?protocol_id=${encodeURIComponent(protocolId)}`
   151→            ),
   152→        enabled: !!protocolId,
   153→    });
   154→}
   155→
   156→export function useBatchCriteria(batchId: string, sortBy?: string, sortOrder?: string) {
   157→    const params = new URLSearchParams();
   158→    if (sortBy) {
   159→        params.set('sort_by', sortBy);
   160→    }
   161→    if (sortOrder) {
   162→        params.set('sort_order', sortOrder);
   163→    }
   164→    const queryString = params.toString();
   165→    const url = queryString
   166→        ? `/reviews/batches/${batchId}/criteria?${queryString}`
   167→        : `/reviews/batches/${batchId}/criteria`;
   168→
   169→    return useQuery({
   170→        queryKey: ['batch-criteria', batchId, sortBy, sortOrder],
   171→        queryFn: () => fetchApi<Criterion[]>(url),
   172→        enabled: Boolean(batchId),
   173→    });
   174→}
   175→
   176→export function useReviewAction() {
   177→    const queryClient = useQueryClient();
   178→
   179→    return useMutation({
   180→        mutationFn: ({ criteriaId, ...body }: ReviewActionRequest & { criteriaId: string }) =>
   181→            fetchApi<Criterion>(`/reviews/criteria/${criteriaId}/action`, {
   182→                method: 'POST',
   183→                body: JSON.stringify(body),
   184→            }),
   185→        onSuccess: () => {
   186→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['batch-criteria'] });
   187→            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['review-batches'] });
   188→        },
   189→    });
   190→}
   191→
   192→export function usePdfUrl(protocolId: string) {
   193→    return useQuery({
   194→        queryKey: ['pdf-url', protocolId],
   195→        queryFn: () => fetchApi<PdfUrlResponse>(`/reviews/protocols/${protocolId}/pdf-url`),
   196→        enabled: Boolean(protocolId),
   197→        staleTime: 50 * 60 * 1000,
   198→    });
   199→}
   200→
   201→export function useAuditLog(
   202→    page: number,
   203→    pageSize: number,
   204→    targetType?: string,
   205→    targetId?: string,
   206→    batchId?: string
   207→) {
   208→    const params = new URLSearchParams({
   209→        page: String(page),
   210→        page_size: String(pageSize),
   211→    });
   212→    if (targetType) {
   213→        params.set('target_type', targetType);
   214→    }
   215→    if (targetId) {
   216→        params.set('target_id', targetId);
   217→    }
   218→    if (batchId) {
   219→        params.set('batch_id', batchId);
   220→    }
   221→
   222→    return useQuery({
   223→        queryKey: ['audit-log', page, pageSize, targetType, targetId, batchId],
   224→        queryFn: () => fetchApi<AuditLogListResponse>(`/reviews/audit-log?${params.toString()}`),
   225→        staleTime: 30_000,
   226→    });
   227→}
   228→
   229→export function usePendingSummary() {
   230→    return useQuery({
   231→        queryKey: ['pending-summary'],
   232→        queryFn: () => fetchApi<PendingSummary>('/reviews/pending-summary'),
   233→        staleTime: 30 * 1000,
   234→    });
   235→}
   236→
   237→export function usePipelineSummary() {
   238→    return useQuery({
   239→        queryKey: ['pipeline-summary'],
   240→        queryFn: () => fetchApi<PipelineSummary>('/reviews/pipeline-summary'),
   241→        staleTime: 30 * 1000,
   242→    });
   243→}
