     1→import type { Criterion } from '../hooks/useReviews';
     2→
     3→interface FieldMappingBadgesProps {
     4→    criterion: Criterion;
     5→    onEditClick?: () => void;
     6→}
     7→
     8→/** Mapping from legacy relation operators to frontend-standard operators. */
     9→const RELATION_DISPLAY_MAP: Record<string, string> = {
    10→    has: 'contains',
    11→    is: '=',
    12→    not: 'not_contains',
    13→    '==': '=',
    14→    range: 'within',
    15→};
    16→
    17→function normalizeRelationDisplay(rel: string): string {
    18→    return RELATION_DISPLAY_MAP[rel] ?? rel;
    19→}
    20→
    21→function formatMappingValue(value: Record<string, unknown>): string {
    22→    if (typeof value !== 'object') return '';
    23→    if (value.type === 'range')
    24→        return `${value.min}–${value.max}${value.unit ? ` ${value.unit}` : ''}`;
    25→    if (value.type === 'temporal') return `${value.duration} ${value.unit}`;
    26→    if (value.type === 'standard') return `${value.value}${value.unit ? ` ${value.unit}` : ''}`;
    27→    return '';
    28→}
    29→
    30→function renderValue(value: unknown): string {
    31→    if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';
    32→    if (typeof value === 'string') return value;
    33→    if (typeof value === 'number') return String(value);
    34→    if (typeof value === 'object') return formatMappingValue(value as Record<string, unknown>);
    35→    return '';
    36→}
    37→
    38→interface RawFieldMapping {
    39→    entity: string;
    40→    entity_code?: string;
    41→    entity_concept_id?: string;
    42→    entity_system?: string;
    43→    entity_concept_system?: string;
    44→    omop_concept_id?: string;
    45→    relation: string;
    46→    value: unknown;
    47→    unit?: string;
    48→}
    49→
    50→export default function FieldMappingBadges({ criterion, onEditClick }: FieldMappingBadgesProps) {
    51→    const cond = criterion.conditions as Record<string, unknown> | null;
    52→    const fieldMappings =
    53→        cond && 'field_mappings' in cond && Array.isArray(cond.field_mappings)
    54→            ? (cond.field_mappings as Array<RawFieldMapping>)
    55→            : null;
    56→
    57→    if (!fieldMappings || fieldMappings.length === 0) return null;
    58→
    59→    const badgeContent = (mapping: RawFieldMapping) => {
    60→        // Legacy key fallbacks
    61→        const entityCode = mapping.entity_code ?? mapping.entity_concept_id;
    62→        const entitySystem = mapping.entity_system ?? mapping.entity_concept_system;
    63→        const relation = normalizeRelationDisplay(mapping.relation);
    64→
    65→        // Handle flat value format (string/number) alongside typed object format
    66→        let valueText: string;
    67→        if (typeof mapping.value === 'string' || typeof mapping.value === 'number') {
    68→            const unit = mapping.unit ? ` ${mapping.unit}` : '';
    69→            valueText = `${mapping.value}${unit}`;
    70→        } else {
    71→            valueText = renderValue(mapping.value);
    72→        }
    73→
    74→        return (
    75→            <>
    76→                <span className="font-semibold text-blue-900">{mapping.entity || '—'}</span>
    77→                {entityCode && (
    78→                    <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-green-50 border border-green-200 px-1.5 py-0 text-[10px] text-green-700">
    79→                        {entitySystem?.toUpperCase()}: {entityCode}
    80→                    </span>
    81→                )}
    82→                {mapping.omop_concept_id && (
    83→                    <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 px-1.5 py-0 text-[10px] text-amber-700">
    84→                        OMOP: {mapping.omop_concept_id}
    85→                    </span>
    86→                )}
    87→                {relation && <span className="text-blue-600 font-mono text-xs">{relation}</span>}
    88→                {relation && valueText ? (
    89→                    <span className="text-blue-800">{valueText}</span>
    90→                ) : relation && !valueText ? (
    91→                    <span className="text-muted-foreground/50 italic text-xs">no value</span>
    92→                ) : null}
    93→            </>
    94→        );
    95→    };
    96→
    97→    return (
    98→        <div className="mb-3">
    99→            <div className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground mb-2">Field Mappings</div>
   100→            <div className="space-y-1">
   101→                {fieldMappings.map((mapping, idx) => (
   102→                    // biome-ignore lint/suspicious/noArrayIndexKey: field_mappings have no stable unique id
   103→                    <div key={idx}>
   104→                        {idx > 0 && (
   105→                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 py-1">
   106→                                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-purple-600 bg-purple-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
   107→                                    AND
   108→                                </span>
   109→                                <div className="flex-1 border-t border-dashed border-muted" />
   110→                            </div>
   111→                        )}
   112→                        {onEditClick ? (
   113→                            <button
   114→                                onClick={onEditClick}
   115→                                className="w-full text-left flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border bg-blue-50/50 border-blue-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-blue-100/50 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
   116→                                title="Click to edit field mappings"
   117→                                type="button"
   118→                            >
   119→                                {badgeContent(mapping)}
   120→                            </button>
   121→                        ) : (
   122→                            <div className="w-full flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border bg-blue-50/50 border-blue-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm">
   123→                                {badgeContent(mapping)}
   124→                            </div>
   125→                        )}
   126→                    </div>
   127→                ))}
   128→            </div>
   129→        </div>
   130→    );
   131→}
   132→
```

     1→from __future__ import annotations
     2→
     3→import json
     4→import logging
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→from typing import Any, Awaitable, Callable, List, Mapping, TypeVar
     7→
     8→from jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader
     9→from pydantic import BaseModel, ValidationError
    10→from shared.lazy_cache import lazy_singleton
    11→from tenacity import retry, stop_after_attempt, wait_exponential
    12→
    13→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    14→TModel = TypeVar("TModel", bound=BaseModel)
    15→
    16→
    17→class AgentError(Exception):
    18→    """Base exception for agent failures."""
    19→
    20→    pass
    21→
    22→
    23→class AgentTimeoutError(AgentError):
    24→    """Agent exceeded max steps or time limit."""
    25→
    26→    pass
    27→
    28→
    29→def render_prompts(
    30→    *,
    31→    prompts_dir: Path,
    32→    system_template: str,
    33→    user_template: str,
    34→    prompt_vars: Mapping[str, Any],
    35→) -> tuple[str, str]:
    36→    """Render Jinja2 templates for system and user prompts."""
    37→    jinja_env = Environment(loader=FileSystemLoader(str(prompts_dir)))
    38→    system_tpl = jinja_env.get_template(system_template)
    39→    user_tpl = jinja_env.get_template(user_template)
    40→    return system_tpl.render(**prompt_vars), user_tpl.render(**prompt_vars)
    41→
    42→
    43→def create_structured_extractor(
    44→    *,
    45→    model_loader: Callable[[], Any],
    46→    prompts_dir: Path,
    47→    response_schema: type[TModel],
    48→    system_template: str,
    49→    user_template: str,
    50→    max_retries: int = 3,
    51→) -> Callable[[Mapping[str, Any]], Awaitable[TModel]]:
    52→    """Create a structured extraction function with retry logic.
    53→
    54→    Args:
    55→        model_loader: Callable returning a LangChain-compatible model.
    56→        prompts_dir: Directory containing Jinja2 templates.
    57→        response_schema: Pydantic model for structured output.
    58→        system_template: Filename of system prompt template.
    59→        user_template: Filename of user prompt template.
    60→        max_retries: Number of retry attempts on failure.
    61→
    62→    Returns:
    63→        Async function that takes prompt variables and returns validated output.
    64→    """
    65→
    66→    @lazy_singleton
    67→    def _get_structured_model() -> Any:
    68→        model = model_loader()
    69→        return model.with_structured_output(response_schema)
    70→
    71→    @retry(
    72→        stop=stop_after_attempt(max_retries),
    73→        wait=wait_exponential(multiplier=1, min=2, max=30),
    74→    )
    75→    async def invoke(prompt_vars: Mapping[str, Any]) -> TModel:
    76→        system_prompt, user_prompt = render_prompts(
    77→            prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
    78→            system_template=system_template,
    79→            user_template=user_template,
    80→            prompt_vars=prompt_vars,
    81→        )
    82→        structured_model = _get_structured_model()
    83→
    84→        try:
    85→            result = await structured_model.ainvoke(
    86→                [("system", system_prompt), ("user", user_prompt)]
    87→            )
    88→        except Exception as e:
    89→            logger.error(f"Model invocation failed: {e}")
    90→            raise AgentError(f"Model invocation failed: {e}") from e
    91→
    92→        # Validate and return
    93→        if isinstance(result, response_schema):
    94→            return result
    95→        if isinstance(result, dict):
    96→            try:
    97→                return response_schema(**result)
    98→            except ValidationError as e:
    99→                logger.warning(f"Validation failed, retrying: {e}")
   100→                raise AgentError(f"Output validation failed: {e}") from e
   101→
   102→        raise AgentError(f"Unexpected output type: {type(result)}")
   103→
   104→    return invoke
   105→
   106→
   107→def create_react_agent_with_tools(
   108→    *,
   109→    model_loader: Callable[[], Any],
   110→    prompts_dir: Path,
   111→    tools: List[Any],
   112→    response_schema: type[TModel],
   113→    system_template: str,
   114→    user_template: str,
   115→    max_steps: int = 10,
   116→) -> Callable[[Mapping[str, Any]], Awaitable[TModel]]:
   117→    """Create a ReAct agent with tools and cycle prevention.
   118→
   119→    Includes hard stop after max_steps to prevent infinite loops.
   120→    """
   121→    from langgraph.prebuilt import create_react_agent
   122→
   123→    @lazy_singleton
   124→    def _get_agent():
   125→        model = model_loader()
   126→        return create_react_agent(model=model, tools=tools)
   127→
   128→    async def invoke(prompt_vars: Mapping[str, Any]) -> TModel:
   129→        system_prompt, user_prompt = render_prompts(
   130→            prompts_dir=prompts_dir,
   131→            system_template=system_template,
   132→            user_template=user_template,
   133→            prompt_vars=prompt_vars,
   134→        )
   135→
   136→        agent = _get_agent()
   137→        config = {"recursion_limit": max_steps}
   138→
   139→        try:
   140→            result = await agent.ainvoke(
   141→                {"messages": [("system", system_prompt), ("user", user_prompt)]},
   142→                config=config,
   143→            )
   144→        except Exception as e:
   145→            if "recursion" in str(e).lower():
   146→                raise AgentTimeoutError(f"Agent exceeded {max_steps} steps") from e
   147→            raise AgentError(str(e)) from e
   148→
   149→        # Extract structured output from final message
   150→        messages = result.get("messages", [])
   151→        if not messages:
   152→            raise AgentError("Agent returned no messages")
   153→
   154→        final_message = messages[-1]
   155→        content = getattr(final_message, "content", str(final_message))
   156→
   157→        # Parse the content as structured output
   158→        try:
   159→            if isinstance(content, dict):
   160→                return response_schema(**content)
   161→            elif isinstance(content, str):
   162→                parsed = json.loads(content)
   163→                return response_schema(**parsed)
   164→            elif isinstance(content, response_schema):
   165→                return content
   166→            else:
   167→                raise AgentError(f"Cannot parse content of type {type(content)}")
   168→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValidationError) as e:
   169→            logger.error(f"Failed to parse agent output: {e}")
   170→            raise AgentError(f"Failed to parse agent output: {e}") from e
   171→
   172→    return invoke
   173→
```

     1→"""Database storage configuration."""

     5→from sqlmodel import SQLModel, create_engine
     6→
     7→# Get database URL from environment -- no fallback
     8→DATABASE_URL = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL")
     9→if not DATABASE_URL:
    10→    raise ValueError(
    11→        "DATABASE_URL environment variable is required but not set. "
    12→        "Set it to your PostgreSQL connection string (e.g., postgresql://user:pass@localhost/dbname)"
    13→    )
    14→
    15→# Create engine with appropriate settings
    16→connect_args: dict = {}
    17→if DATABASE_URL.startswith("sqlite"):
    18→    connect_args["check_same_thread"] = False
    19→engine = create_engine(DATABASE_URL, connect_args=connect_args, echo=False)
    20→
    21→
    22→def create_db_and_tables() -> None:
    23→    """Create all database tables."""
    24→    SQLModel.metadata.create_all(engine)

     1→"""Shared data models for the clinical trial criteria extraction system."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from datetime import datetime
     6→from enum import Enum
     7→from typing import Any, Dict
     8→from uuid import uuid4
     9→
    10→from sqlalchemy import (
    11→    JSON,
    12→    Boolean,
    13→    Column,
    14→    DateTime,
    15→    Float,
    16→    Index,
    17→    Integer,
    18→    String,
    19→    Text,
    20→    func,
    21→)
    22→from sqlmodel import Field, SQLModel
    23→
    24→
    25→def _ts_col() -> Column:  # type: ignore[type-arg]
    26→    """Create a created_at timestamp column with server default."""
    27→    return Column(
    28→        DateTime(timezone=True),
    29→        server_default=func.now(),
    30→        nullable=False,
    31→    )
    32→
    33→
    34→def _ts_col_update() -> Column:  # type: ignore[type-arg]
    35→    """Create an updated_at timestamp column with server default and onupdate."""
    36→    return Column(
    37→        DateTime(timezone=True),
    38→        server_default=func.now(),
    39→        onupdate=func.now(),
    40→        nullable=False,
    41→    )
    42→
    43→
    44→class ProtocolStatus(str, Enum):
    45→    """Protocol processing status enum."""
    46→
    47→    UPLOADED = "uploaded"
    48→    PENDING = "pending"
    49→    EXTRACTING = "extracting"
    50→    EXTRACTION_FAILED = "extraction_failed"
    51→    GROUNDING = "grounding"
    52→    GROUNDING_FAILED = "grounding_failed"
    53→    PENDING_REVIEW = "pending_review"
    54→    COMPLETE = "complete"
    55→    DEAD_LETTER = "dead_letter"
    56→    ARCHIVED = "archived"
    57→
    58→
    59→class Protocol(SQLModel, table=True):
    60→    """Uploaded clinical trial protocol PDF."""
    61→
    62→    id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()), primary_key=True)
    63→    title: str = Field()
    64→    file_uri: str = Field()
    65→    status: str = Field(default=ProtocolStatus.UPLOADED, index=True)
    66→    page_count: int | None = Field(default=None)
    67→    quality_score: float | None = Field(default=None)
    68→    error_reason: str | None = Field(default=None)
    69→    metadata_: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict, sa_column=Column(JSON))
    70→    created_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col())
    71→    updated_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col_update())
    72→
    73→
    74→class CriteriaBatch(SQLModel, table=True):
    75→    """A batch of criteria extracted from a protocol."""
    76→
    77→    id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()), primary_key=True)
    78→    protocol_id: str = Field(foreign_key="protocol.id", index=True)
    79→    status: str = Field(default="pending_review", index=True)
    80→    extraction_model: str | None = Field(default=None)
    81→    created_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col())
    82→    updated_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col_update())
    83→    is_archived: bool = Field(default=False, index=True)
    84→
    85→
    86→class Criteria(SQLModel, table=True):
    87→    """Individual inclusion/exclusion criterion."""
    88→
    89→    id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()), primary_key=True)
    90→    batch_id: str = Field(foreign_key="criteriabatch.id", index=True)
    91→    criteria_type: str = Field()
    92→    category: str | None = Field(default=None)
    93→    text: str = Field(sa_column=Column(Text, nullable=False))
    94→    temporal_constraint: Dict[str, Any] | None = Field(
    95→        default=None, sa_column=Column(JSON)
    96→    )
    97→    conditions: Dict[str, Any] | None = Field(default=None, sa_column=Column(JSON))
    98→    numeric_thresholds: Dict[str, Any] | None = Field(
    99→        default=None, sa_column=Column(JSON)
   100→    )
   101→    assertion_status: str | None = Field(default=None)
   102→    confidence: float = Field(default=1.0)
   103→    source_section: str | None = Field(default=None)
   104→    page_number: int | None = Field(default=None)
   105→    review_status: str | None = Field(default=None)
   106→    # Phase 2: expression tree JSONB

   111→    updated_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col_update())
   112→
   113→

   182→
   183→
   184→class AtomicCriterion(SQLModel, table=True):
   185→    """Leaf node in a criterion expression tree.
   186→
   187→    Represents a single atomic condition (e.g. "HbA1c >= 7%") decomposed
   188→    from a criterion's field_mappings. Links to the parent Criteria record
   189→    and carries the OMOP concept ID for direct CDM joins.
   190→
   191→    Table: atomic_criteria
   192→    """
   193→
   194→    __tablename__ = "atomic_criteria"  # type: ignore[assignment]
   195→    __table_args__ = (
   196→        Index("ix_atomic_proto_incl", "protocol_id", "inclusion_exclusion"),
   197→    )
   198→
   199→    id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()), primary_key=True)
   200→    criterion_id: str = Field(foreign_key="criteria.id", index=True)
   201→    protocol_id: str = Field(foreign_key="protocol.id", index=True)
   202→    inclusion_exclusion: str = Field()
   203→    entity_concept_id: str | None = Field(default=None, index=True)
   204→    entity_concept_system: str | None = Field(default=None)
   205→    omop_concept_id: str | None = Field(default=None, index=True)
   206→    entity_domain: str | None = Field(default=None)
   207→    relation_operator: str | None = Field(default=None)
   208→    value_numeric: float | None = Field(
   209→        default=None, sa_column=Column(Float, nullable=True)
   210→    )
   211→    value_text: str | None = Field(default=None)
   212→    unit_text: str | None = Field(default=None)
   213→    unit_concept_id: int | None = Field(
   214→        default=None, sa_column=Column(Integer, nullable=True)
   215→    )
   216→    value_concept_id: int | None = Field(
   217→        default=None, sa_column=Column(Integer, nullable=True)
   218→    )
   219→    negation: bool = Field(default=False, sa_column=Column(Boolean, default=False))
   220→    temporal_constraint: Dict[str, Any] | None = Field(
   221→        default=None, sa_column=Column(JSON)
   222→    )
   223→    original_text: str | None = Field(default=None)
   224→    confidence_score: float | None = Field(default=None)
   225→    human_verified: bool = Field(
   226→        default=False, sa_column=Column(Boolean, default=False)
   227→    )
   228→    human_modified: bool = Field(
   229→        default=False, sa_column=Column(Boolean, default=False)
   230→    )
   231→    created_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col())
   232→    updated_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col_update())
   233→
   234→
   235→class CompositeCriterion(SQLModel, table=True):
   236→    """Branch node in a criterion expression tree.
   237→
   238→    Represents a logical operator (AND/OR/NOT) that combines child nodes.
   239→    Supports nested composition via parent_criterion_id self-reference.
   240→
   241→    Table: composite_criteria
   242→    """
   243→
   244→    __tablename__ = "composite_criteria"  # type: ignore[assignment]
   245→
   246→    id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()), primary_key=True)
   247→    criterion_id: str = Field(foreign_key="criteria.id", index=True)
   248→    protocol_id: str = Field(foreign_key="protocol.id", index=True)
   249→    inclusion_exclusion: str = Field()
   250→    logic_operator: str = Field()
   251→    # Reserved for future HITL use. Tree parent-child relationships are
   252→    # stored in CriterionRelationship; this field is intentionally unused
   253→    # by the automated pipeline.
   254→    parent_criterion_id: str | None = Field(default=None, index=True)
   255→    original_text: str | None = Field(default=None)
   256→    human_verified: bool = Field(
   257→        default=False, sa_column=Column(Boolean, default=False)
   258→    )
   259→    created_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col())
   260→    updated_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col_update())
   261→
   262→
   263→class CriterionRelationship(SQLModel, table=True):
   264→    """Edge in a criterion expression tree.
   265→
   266→    Links a parent CompositeCriterion to a child (atomic or composite)
   267→    via polymorphic FK. child_type discriminates between the two tables.
   268→    child_sequence preserves operand order.
   269→
   270→    Table: criterion_relationships
   271→    """
   272→
   273→    __tablename__ = "criterion_relationships"  # type: ignore[assignment]
   274→
   275→    parent_criterion_id: str = Field(
   276→        foreign_key="composite_criteria.id", primary_key=True
   277→    )
   278→    child_criterion_id: str = Field(primary_key=True)
   279→    child_type: str = Field()
   280→    child_sequence: int = Field(
   281→        default=0, sa_column=Column(Integer, default=0, nullable=False)
   282→    )
   283→
   284→
   285→class OutboxEvent(SQLModel, table=True):
   286→    """Transactional outbox for event publishing."""
   287→
   288→    id: str = Field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()), primary_key=True)
   289→    event_type: str = Field(index=True)
   290→    aggregate_type: str = Field()
   291→    aggregate_id: str = Field(index=True)
   292→    payload: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict, sa_column=Column(JSON))
   293→    idempotency_key: str = Field(sa_column=Column(String, unique=True, nullable=False))
   294→    status: str = Field(default="pending", index=True)
   295→    retry_count: int = Field(default=0)
   296→    published_at: datetime | None = Field(default=None)
   297→    created_at: datetime = Field(sa_column=_ts_col())
   298→

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/libs/inference/src/inference/adapters/base.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Base adapter ABC for provider-agnostic inference.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\n\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel\n\n\nclass BaseAdapter(ABC):\n    \"\"\"Provider-agnostic inference adapter.\"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    async def upload_file(self, file_bytes: bytes, filename: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Upload file, return file_id.\n\n        Remote adapters use provider File API.\n        Local adapters store to disk and return local file_id.\n        \"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    async def generate(\n        self,\n        file_id: str | None,\n        input_text: str,\n        system_prompt: str,\n    ) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Generate text response.\n\n        For remote: passes provider file_id in request.\n        For local: retrieves stored file, extracts text, feeds to LLM.\n        \"\"\"\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    async def generate_structured(\n        self,\n        file_id: str | None,\n        input_text: str,\n        system_prompt: str,\n        output_schema: type[BaseModel],\n    ) -> BaseModel:\n        \"\"\"Generate structured output.\n\n        Remote: native structured output.\n        Local: JSON mode + Pydantic validation.\n        \"\"\"\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/libs/inference/src/inference/adapters/gemini_adapter.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Remote Gemini adapter: maps file_id to Gemini File API, uses google-genai SDK.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nfrom typing import Any, cast\n\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel\n\nfrom inference.adapters.base import BaseAdapter\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass GeminiAdapter(BaseAdapter):\n    \"\"\"Adapter for Google Gemini API (remote).\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        api_key: str | None = None,\n        model_name: str | None = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        self._api_key = api_key or os.getenv(\"GOOGLE_API_KEY\", \"\")\n        self._model_name = model_name or os.getenv(\n            \"GEMINI_MODEL_NAME\", \"gemini-2.5-flash\"\n        )\n        self._file_map: dict[str, str] = {}  # gateway_file_id -> gemini_file_name\n\n    def _get_client(self) -> Any:\n        from google import genai\n\n        return genai.Client(api_key=self._api_key)\n\n    async def upload_file(self, file_bytes: bytes, filename: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Upload to Gemini File API, return gateway file_id.\"\"\"\n        import tempfile\n        from uuid import uuid4\n\n        client = self._get_client()\n        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(\n            suffix=f\"_{filename}\", delete=False\n        ) as tmp:\n            tmp.write(file_bytes)\n            tmp_path = tmp.name\n\n        try:\n            uploaded = client.files.upload(file=tmp_path)\n            gateway_id = str(uuid4())\n            self._file_map[gateway_id] = uploaded.name\n            logger.info(\n                \"Uploaded file to Gemini: %s -> %s\", gateway_id, uploaded.name\n            )\n            return gateway_id\n        finally:\n            import os as _os\n\n            try:\n                _os.unlink(tmp_path)\n            except OSError:\n                pass\n\n    async def generate(\n        self,\n        file_id: str | None,\n        input_text: str,\n        system_prompt: str,\n    ) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Generate text via Gemini API.\"\"\"\n        from google.genai import types\n\n        client = self._get_client()\n        contents: list[Any] = []\n\n        if file_id and file_id in self._file_map:\n            gemini_name = self._file_map[file_id]\n            gemini_file = client.files.get(name=gemini_name)\n            contents.append(gemini_file)\n\n        contents.append(input_text)\n\n        response = await client.aio.models.generate_content(\n            model=self._model_name,\n            contents=cast(Any, contents),\n            config=types.GenerateContentConfig(\n                system_instruction=system_prompt,\n            ),\n        )\n        return response.text or \"\"\n\n    async def generate_structured(\n        self,\n        file_id: str | None,\n        input_text: str,\n        system_prompt: str,\n        output_schema: type[BaseModel],\n    ) -> BaseModel:\n        \"\"\"Generate structured output via Gemini API.\"\"\"\n        from google.genai import types\n\n        client = self._get_client()\n        contents: list[Any] = []\n\n        if file_id and file_id in self._file_map:\n            gemini_name = self._file_map[file_id]\n            gemini_file = client.files.get(name=gemini_name)\n            contents.append(gemini_file)\n\n        contents.append(input_text)\n\n        response = await client.aio.models.generate_content(\n            model=self._model_name,\n            contents=cast(Any, contents),\n            config=types.GenerateContentConfig(\n                system_instruction=system_prompt,\n                response_mime_type=\"application/json\",\n                response_schema=output_schema,\n            ),\n        )\n\n        if response.parsed is not None:\n            return cast(BaseModel, response.parsed)\n\n        text = response.text or \"\"\n        return output_schema.model_validate_json(text)\n\n    def cleanup_file(self, file_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Delete uploaded file from Gemini File API.\"\"\"\n        if file_id in self._file_map:\n            try:\n                client = self._get_client()\n                client.files.delete(name=self._file_map[file_id])\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.warning(\"Failed to cleanup Gemini file %s: %s\", file_id, e)\n            finally:\n                del self._file_map[file_id]\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/libs/inference/src/inference/adapters/vertex_adapter.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Remote Vertex adapter: uses existing ModelGardenChatModel.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel\n\nfrom inference.adapters.base import BaseAdapter\nfrom inference.config import AgentConfig\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass VertexAdapter(BaseAdapter):\n    \"\"\"Adapter for Vertex AI Model Garden endpoints.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config: AgentConfig | None = None) -> None:\n        from inference.model_garden import create_model_loader\n\n        self._config = config or AgentConfig.from_env()\n        self._loader = create_model_loader(self._config)\n\n    async def upload_file(self, file_bytes: bytes, filename: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Vertex endpoints don't use file uploads; store locally.\"\"\"\n        from uuid import uuid4\n\n        from inference.file_store import FileStore\n\n        store = FileStore()\n        file_id = str(uuid4())\n        store.store(file_id, file_bytes, filename)\n        return file_id\n\n    async def generate(\n        self,\n        file_id: str | None,\n        input_text: str,\n        system_prompt: str,\n    ) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Generate via Vertex AI endpoint.\"\"\"\n        from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, SystemMessage\n\n        model = self._loader()\n        messages: list[Any] = [SystemMessage(content=system_prompt)]\n\n        if file_id:\n            from inference.file_store import FileStore\n\n            store = FileStore()\n            text_content = store.get_text(file_id)\n            if text_content:\n                input_text = f\"Document content:\\n{text_content}\\n\\n{input_text}\"\n\n        messages.append(HumanMessage(content=input_text))\n        result = await model.ainvoke(messages)\n        return str(result.content)\n\n    async def generate_structured(\n        self,\n        file_id: str | None,\n        input_text: str,\n        system_prompt: str,\n        output_schema: type[BaseModel],\n    ) -> BaseModel:\n        \"\"\"Generate structured output via Vertex AI.\"\"\"\n        from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, SystemMessage\n\n        model = self._loader()\n        structured = model.with_structured_output(output_schema)\n\n        messages: list[Any] = [SystemMessage(content=system_prompt)]\n\n        if file_id:\n            from inference.file_store import FileStore\n\n            store = FileStore()\n            text_content = store.get_text(file_id)\n            if text_content:\n                input_text = f\"Document content:\\n{text_content}\\n\\n{input_text}\"\n\n        messages.append(HumanMessage(content=input_text))\n        result = await structured.ainvoke(messages)\n\n        if isinstance(result, output_schema):\n            return result\n        if isinstance(result, dict):\n            return output_schema.model_validate(result)\n        return result  # type: ignore[return-value]\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/libs/inference/src/inference/adapters/ollama_adapter.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Local Ollama adapter: stores PDFs locally, extracts text, feeds to Ollama.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport os\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel\n\nfrom inference.adapters.base import BaseAdapter\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass OllamaAdapter(BaseAdapter):\n    \"\"\"Adapter for local Ollama inference.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        base_url: str | None = None,\n        model: str | None = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        self._base_url = base_url or os.getenv(\n            \"OLLAMA_BASE_URL\", \"http://localhost:11434\"\n        )\n        self._model = model or os.getenv(\"OLLAMA_MODEL\", \"gemma2:9b\")\n\n    async def upload_file(self, file_bytes: bytes, filename: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Store PDF locally and return file_id.\"\"\"\n        from uuid import uuid4\n\n        from inference.file_store import FileStore\n\n        store = FileStore()\n        file_id = str(uuid4())\n        store.store(file_id, file_bytes, filename)\n        logger.info(\"Stored file locally: %s (%s)\", file_id, filename)\n        return file_id\n\n    def _extract_text(self, file_id: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Extract text from stored PDF via pymupdf4llm.\"\"\"\n        from inference.file_store import FileStore\n\n        store = FileStore()\n        file_bytes = store.get(file_id)\n        if file_bytes is None:\n            raise FileNotFoundError(f\"File {file_id} not found in store\")\n\n        try:\n            import pymupdf4llm\n\n            return pymupdf4llm.to_markdown(file_bytes)\n        except ImportError:\n            import pymupdf\n\n            doc = pymupdf.open(stream=file_bytes, filetype=\"pdf\")\n            return \"\\n\\n\".join(page.get_text() for page in doc)\n\n    async def _call_ollama(\n        self,\n        prompt: str,\n        system_prompt: str,\n        json_mode: bool = False,\n    ) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Call Ollama chat API.\"\"\"\n        import httpx\n\n        messages = [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system_prompt},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": prompt},\n        ]\n\n        payload: dict[str, Any] = {\n            \"model\": self._model,\n            \"messages\": messages,\n            \"stream\": False,\n        }\n        if json_mode:\n            payload[\"format\"] = \"json\"\n\n        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=300.0) as client:\n            response = await client.post(\n                f\"{self._base_url}/api/chat\",\n                json=payload,\n            )\n            response.raise_for_status()\n            data = response.json()\n            return data[\"message\"][\"content\"]\n\n    async def generate(\n        self,\n        file_id: str | None,\n        input_text: str,\n        system_prompt: str,\n    ) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Generate text via Ollama.\"\"\"\n        prompt = input_text\n        if file_id:\n            text_content = self._extract_text(file_id)\n            prompt = f\"Document content:\\n{text_content}\\n\\n{input_text}\"\n\n        return await self._call_ollama(prompt, system_prompt)\n\n    async def generate_structured(\n        self,\n        file_id: str | None,\n        input_text: str,\n        system_prompt: str,\n        output_schema: type[BaseModel],\n    ) -> BaseModel:\n        \"\"\"Generate structured output via Ollama JSON mode + Pydantic validation.\"\"\"\n        prompt = input_text\n        if file_id:\n            text_content = self._extract_text(file_id)\n            prompt = f\"Document content:\\n{text_content}\\n\\n{input_text}\"\n\n        schema_json = json.dumps(output_schema.model_json_schema(), indent=2)\n        structured_prompt = (\n            f\"{prompt}\\n\\nRespond with valid JSON matching this schema:\\n{schema_json}\"\n        )\n\n        result = await self._call_ollama(\n            structured_prompt, system_prompt, json_mode=True\n        )\n        return output_schema.model_validate_json(result)\n"
}

  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/libs/inference/src/inference/file_store.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Local file store for the inference gateway.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n_STORE_DIR = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / \"inference_file_store\"\n\n\nclass FileStore:\n    \"\"\"Simple local file store for PDF and text content.\n\n    Stores files on disk keyed by file_id. Provides both raw bytes\n    and extracted text retrieval.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, base_dir: Path | None = None) -> None:\n        self._base_dir = base_dir or _STORE_DIR\n        self._base_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    def store(self, file_id: str, data: bytes, filename: str) -> Path:\n        \"\"\"Store file bytes and return the path.\"\"\"\n        file_path = self._base_dir / f\"{file_id}_{filename}\"\n        file_path.write_bytes(data)\n        return file_path\n\n    def get(self, file_id: str) -> bytes | None:\n        \"\"\"Retrieve file bytes by file_id prefix.\"\"\"\n        for path in self._base_dir.iterdir():\n            if path.name.startswith(file_id):\n                return path.read_bytes()\n        return None\n\n    def get_text(self, file_id: str) -> str | None:\n        \"\"\"Retrieve file and extract text (PDF -> markdown).\"\"\"\n        file_bytes = self.get(file_id)\n        if file_bytes is None:\n            return None\n\n        try:\n            import pymupdf4llm\n\n            return pymupdf4llm.to_markdown(file_bytes)\n        except (ImportError, Exception):\n            try:\n                import pymupdf\n\n                doc = pymupdf.open(stream=file_bytes, filetype=\"pdf\")\n                return \"\\n\\n\".join(page.get_text() for page in doc)\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.warning(\"Failed to extract text from file %s: %s\", file_id, e)\n                return file_bytes.decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n\n    def delete(self, file_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Delete stored file.\"\"\"\n        for path in self._base_dir.iterdir():\n            if path.name.startswith(file_id):\n                path.unlink(missing_ok=True)\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/libs/inference/src/inference/router.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Model router: maps logical model names to provider adapters based on config.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n\nfrom inference.adapters.base import BaseAdapter\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass AdapterConfig:\n    \"\"\"Configuration for a single adapter mapping.\"\"\"\n\n    provider: str  # \"gemini\", \"vertex\", \"ollama\"\n    model: str | None = None\n    base_url: str | None = None\n\n\nclass ModelRouter:\n    \"\"\"Routes logical model names to adapters based on config.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config: dict[str, AdapterConfig] | None = None) -> None:\n        self._config = config or self._default_config()\n        self._adapters: dict[str, BaseAdapter] = {}\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _default_config() -> dict[str, AdapterConfig]:\n        \"\"\"Build default config from environment variables.\"\"\"\n        backend = os.getenv(\"MODEL_BACKEND\", \"gemini\").lower()\n\n        if backend == \"ollama\":\n            return {\n                \"extraction\": AdapterConfig(\n                    provider=\"ollama\",\n                    model=os.getenv(\"OLLAMA_EXTRACTION_MODEL\", \"qwen2.5:14b\"),\n                ),\n                \"grounding\": AdapterConfig(\n                    provider=\"ollama\",\n                    model=os.getenv(\"OLLAMA_MODEL\", \"gemma2:9b\"),\n                ),\n                \"structuring\": AdapterConfig(\n                    provider=\"ollama\",\n                    model=os.getenv(\"OLLAMA_MODEL\", \"gemma2:9b\"),\n                ),\n            }\n        elif backend == \"vertex\":\n            return {\n                \"extraction\": AdapterConfig(provider=\"vertex\"),\n                \"grounding\": AdapterConfig(provider=\"vertex\"),\n                \"structuring\": AdapterConfig(provider=\"vertex\"),\n            }\n        else:\n            # Default: gemini for extraction, gemini for structuring\n            return {\n                \"extraction\": AdapterConfig(provider=\"gemini\"),\n                \"grounding\": AdapterConfig(provider=\"gemini\"),\n                \"structuring\": AdapterConfig(provider=\"gemini\"),\n            }\n\n    def _create_adapter(self, cfg: AdapterConfig) -> BaseAdapter:\n        \"\"\"Create adapter instance from config.\"\"\"\n        if cfg.provider == \"ollama\":\n            from inference.adapters.ollama_adapter import OllamaAdapter\n\n            return OllamaAdapter(\n                base_url=cfg.base_url,\n                model=cfg.model,\n            )\n        elif cfg.provider == \"vertex\":\n            from inference.adapters.vertex_adapter import VertexAdapter\n\n            return VertexAdapter()\n        else:\n            from inference.adapters.gemini_adapter import GeminiAdapter\n\n            return GeminiAdapter(model_name=cfg.model)\n\n    def get_adapter(self, role: str) -> BaseAdapter:\n        \"\"\"Get adapter for a logical role.\"\"\"\n        if role not in self._adapters:\n            if role not in self._config:\n                raise ValueError(\n                    f\"No adapter configured for role '{role}'. \"\n                    f\"Available: {list(self._config.keys())}\"\n                )\n            self._adapters[role] = self._create_adapter(self._config[role])\n        return self._adapters[role]\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/libs/inference/src/inference/gateway.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Core inference gateway: unified entry point for all model interactions.\n\nApp code uses InferenceGateway, never provider SDKs directly.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\n\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel\n\nfrom inference.file_store import FileStore\nfrom inference.router import ModelRouter\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass InferenceGateway:\n    \"\"\"Unified entry point for model inference.\n\n    Routes requests to the appropriate adapter based on role.\n    Manages file storage across all providers.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        router: ModelRouter | None = None,\n        file_store: FileStore | None = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        self._router = router or ModelRouter()\n        self._file_store = file_store or FileStore()\n\n    async def upload_file(self, file_bytes: bytes, filename: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Store file and return gateway file_id.\n\n        The file is stored in the local file store. When an adapter\n        needs to upload to a remote provider, it handles that internally.\n        \"\"\"\n        from uuid import uuid4\n\n        file_id = str(uuid4())\n        self._file_store.store(file_id, file_bytes, filename)\n        logger.info(\"Gateway stored file: %s (%s)\", file_id, filename)\n        return file_id\n\n    async def generate(\n        self,\n        role: str,\n        file_id: str | None,\n        input_text: str,\n        system_prompt: str,\n    ) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Route text generation to appropriate adapter by role.\"\"\"\n        adapter = self._router.get_adapter(role)\n\n        # For remote adapters that need their own file upload\n        adapter_file_id = None\n        if file_id:\n            file_bytes = self._file_store.get(file_id)\n            if file_bytes:\n                adapter_file_id = await adapter.upload_file(file_bytes, f\"{file_id}.pdf\")\n\n        return await adapter.generate(adapter_file_id, input_text, system_prompt)\n\n    async def generate_structured(\n        self,\n        role: str,\n        file_id: str | None,\n        input_text: str,\n        system_prompt: str,\n        output_schema: type[BaseModel],\n    ) -> BaseModel:\n        \"\"\"Route structured generation to appropriate adapter by role.\"\"\"\n        adapter = self._router.get_adapter(role)\n\n        adapter_file_id = None\n        if file_id:\n            file_bytes = self._file_store.get(file_id)\n            if file_bytes:\n                adapter_file_id = await adapter.upload_file(file_bytes, f\"{file_id}.pdf\")\n\n        return await adapter.generate_structured(\n            adapter_file_id, input_text, system_prompt, output_schema\n        )\n\n    def cleanup(self, file_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Clean up stored file.\"\"\"\n        self._file_store.delete(file_id)\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/gemini_utils.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Shared Gemini LLM utilities for protocol processor tools.\n\nCentralizes the repeated pattern of creating a ChatGoogleGenerativeAI\nclient with structured output and parsing its response. Used by\nstructure_builder, ordinal_resolver, and field_mapper.\n\nFalls back to Ollama when GOOGLE_API_KEY is not set but OLLAMA_BASE_URL\nis configured.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nfrom typing import Any, TypeVar\n\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nT = TypeVar(\"T\", bound=BaseModel)\n\n\ndef create_structured_llm(\n    output_schema: type[T],\n) -> Any | None:\n    \"\"\"Create an LLM client with structured output.\n\n    Tries Gemini first (GOOGLE_API_KEY), falls back to Ollama\n    (OLLAMA_BASE_URL) if Gemini is not configured.\n\n    Args:\n        output_schema: Pydantic model class for structured output.\n\n    Returns:\n        A structured LLM instance, or None if no backend is available.\n    \"\"\"\n    google_api_key = os.getenv(\"GOOGLE_API_KEY\")\n    if google_api_key:\n        try:\n            from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI\n\n            gemini_model_name = os.getenv(\"GEMINI_MODEL_NAME\", \"gemini-2.5-flash\")\n            gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(\n                model=gemini_model_name,\n                google_api_key=google_api_key,\n            )\n            return gemini.with_structured_output(output_schema)\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.warning(\"Failed to create Gemini client: %s\", e)\n\n    # Fallback to Ollama\n    ollama_base_url = os.getenv(\"OLLAMA_BASE_URL\")\n    if ollama_base_url:\n        try:\n            from langchain_community.chat_models import ChatOllama\n\n            ollama_model = os.getenv(\"OLLAMA_MODEL\", \"gemma2:9b\")\n            logger.info(\n                \"Using Ollama for structured output: %s/%s\",\n                ollama_base_url,\n                ollama_model,\n            )\n            ollama = ChatOllama(\n                base_url=ollama_base_url,\n                model=ollama_model,\n                format=\"json\",\n                temperature=0.1,\n            )\n            return ollama.with_structured_output(output_schema)\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.warning(\"Failed to create Ollama client: %s\", e)\n\n    logger.warning(\"No LLM backend available — skipping structured output call\")\n    return None\n\n\ndef parse_structured_output(result: Any, model: type[T]) -> T:\n    \"\"\"Parse a structured LLM output into a Pydantic model.\n\n    Handles both dict and already-parsed model instances returned\n    by LangChain's with_structured_output().\n\n    Args:\n        result: Raw LLM output (dict or model instance).\n        model: Pydantic model class to validate against.\n\n    Returns:\n        Validated model instance.\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(result, dict):\n        return model.model_validate(result)\n    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]\n"
}

LOCAL_EXTRACTION_ENABLED=true, routes through InferenceGateway\n    instead of direct google.genai.Client.\n\n    Returns JSON string (not dict) to minimize LangGraph state size.\n\n    Args:\n        pdf_bytes: Raw PDF bytes to extract criteria from.\n        protocol_id: UUID of the protocol (for logging).\n        title: Protocol title (used in user prompt).\n\n    Returns:\n        JSON string representation of ExtractionResult.\n\n    Raises:\n        ValidationError: If Gemini response cannot be parsed as ExtractionResult.\n        Exception: On Gemini API or File API errors after retries exhausted.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Route through gateway when local extraction is enabled\n    if os.getenv(\"LOCAL_EXTRACTION_ENABLED\", \"\").lower() == \"true\":\n        return await _extract_via_gateway(pdf_bytes, protocol_id, title)\n\n    tmp_path = None\n    uploaded_file = None\n    client = None\n\n    try:\n        # Instantiate client\n        client = genai.Client(api_key=os.getenv(\"GOOGLE_API_KEY\"))\n        model_name = os.getenv(\"GEMINI_MODEL_NAME\", \"gemini-2.5-flash\")\n\n        # Write PDF to temp file for File API upload\n        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=\".pdf\", delete=False) as tmp:\n            tmp.write(pdf_bytes)\n            tmp_path = tmp.name\n\n        # Upload via File API\n        uploaded_file = client.files.upload(file=tmp_path)\n\n        system_prompt, user_prompt = render_prompts(\n            prompts_dir=PROMPTS_DIR,\n            system_template=\"system.jinja2\",\n            user_template=\"user.jinja2\",\n            prompt_vars={\n                \"title\": title,\n            },\n        )\n\n        extraction_result = await _invoke_gemini(\n            client, model_name, uploaded_file, system_prompt, user_prompt\n        )\n\n        logger.info(\n            \"Extracted %d criteria from protocol %s (Gemini File API)\",\n            len(extraction_result.criteria),\n            protocol_id,\n        )\n\n        # Return as JSON string (not dict) for minimal state\n        return extraction_result.model_dump_json()\n\n    except ValidationError as e:\n        msg = _format_validation_error(e)\n        logger.error(\n            \"Extraction validation failed for protocol %s: %s\",\n            protocol_id,\n            msg,\n        )\n        raise\n\n    except Exception:\n        logger.exception(\n            \"Extraction failed for protocol %s\",\n            protocol_id,\n        )\n        raise\n\n    finally:\n        # Clean up temp file\n        if tmp_path:\n            try:\n                os.unlink(tmp_path)\n            except Exception as cleanup_err:\n                logger.warning(\n                    \"Failed to delete temp file %s: %s\", tmp_path, cleanup_err\n                )\n\n        # Clean up uploaded file\n        if uploaded_file and client and uploaded_file.name:\n            try:\n                client.files.delete(name=uploaded_file.name)\n            except Exception as cleanup_err:\n                logger.warning(\n                    \"Failed to delete uploaded file %s: %s\",\n                    uploaded_file.name,\n                    cleanup_err,\n                )"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/scripts/setup-local-models.sh",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\nset -euo pipefail\n\n# ==============================================================================\n# ElixirTrials Local Model Setup\n# Detects hardware, installs Ollama, pulls models, configures .env.local\n# ==============================================================================\n\necho \"=== ElixirTrials Local Model Setup ===\"\necho \"\"\n\n# --- 1. Detect hardware ---\necho \"[1/7] Detecting hardware...\"\nif [[ \"$(uname -s)\" == \"Darwin\" ]]; then\n    if [[ \"$(uname -m)\" == \"arm64\" ]]; then\n        HW=\"apple_silicon\"\n        echo \"  Detected: Apple Silicon (M-series)\"\n    else\n        HW=\"cpu\"\n        echo \"  Detected: Intel Mac (CPU only)\"\n    fi\nelif command -v nvidia-smi &>/dev/null; then\n    HW=\"nvidia\"\n    echo \"  Detected: NVIDIA GPU\"\n    nvidia-smi --query-gpu=name,memory.total --format=csv,noheader 2>/dev/null || true\nelse\n    HW=\"cpu\"\n    echo \"  Detected: CPU only\"\nfi\necho \"\"\n\n# --- 2. Check Ollama ---\necho \"[2/7] Checking Ollama installation...\"\nif command -v ollama &>/dev/null; then\n    echo \"  Ollama is installed: $(ollama --version 2>/dev/null || echo 'version unknown')\"\nelse\n    echo \"  Ollama not found. Installing...\"\n    if [[ \"$(uname -s)\" == \"Darwin\" ]]; then\n        if command -v brew &>/dev/null; then\n            brew install ollama\n        else\n            echo \"  Please install Homebrew first, or install Ollama from https://ollama.ai\"\n            exit 1\n        fi\n    else\n        curl -fsSL https://ollama.ai/install.sh | sh\n    fi\n    echo \"  Ollama installed successfully.\"\nfi\necho \"\"\n\n# --- 3. Start Ollama serve ---\necho \"[3/7] Starting Ollama server...\"\nif curl -s http://localhost:11434/api/tags &>/dev/null; then\n    echo \"  Ollama server already running.\"\nelse\n    ollama serve &>/dev/null &\n    OLLAMA_PID=$!\n    echo \"  Started Ollama server (PID: $OLLAMA_PID)\"\n    sleep 3\n    if ! curl -s http://localhost:11434/api/tags &>/dev/null; then\n        echo \"  WARNING: Ollama server may not have started. Continuing anyway...\"\n    fi\nfi\necho \"\"\n\n# --- 4. Pull grounding model ---\necho \"[4/7] Pulling grounding model...\"\nGROUNDING_MODEL=\"gemma2:9b\"\necho \"  Note: MedGemma 4B-IT is a gated HuggingFace model not yet available in Ollama.\"\necho \"  Using $GROUNDING_MODEL as fallback (strong medical knowledge).\"\necho \"  To use MedGemma when GGUF becomes available:\"\necho \"    1. Download GGUF from HuggingFace\"\necho \"    2. Create Modelfile: FROM ./medgemma-4b-it.gguf\"\necho \"    3. ollama create medgemma -f Modelfile\"\necho \"\"\nollama pull \"$GROUNDING_MODEL\"\necho \"\"\n\n# --- 5. Pull extraction model ---\necho \"[5/7] Pulling extraction model (JSON-capable)...\"\nif [[ \"$HW\" == \"cpu\" ]]; then\n    EXTRACTION_MODEL=\"llama3.1:8b\"\n    echo \"  CPU detected — using smaller model: $EXTRACTION_MODEL\"\nelse\n    EXTRACTION_MODEL=\"qwen2.5:14b\"\n    echo \"  GPU detected — using: $EXTRACTION_MODEL\"\nfi\nollama pull \"$EXTRACTION_MODEL\"\necho \"\"\n\n# --- 6. Write .env.local ---\necho \"[6/7] Writing configuration to .env.local...\"\nENV_FILE=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")/..\" && pwd)/.env.local\"\ncat > \"$ENV_FILE\" <<EOF\n# ElixirTrials Local Model Configuration\n# Generated by setup-local-models.sh on $(date -u +\"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\")\nMODEL_BACKEND=ollama\nOLLAMA_BASE_URL=http://localhost:11434\nOLLAMA_MODEL=${GROUNDING_MODEL}\nOLLAMA_EXTRACTION_MODEL=${EXTRACTION_MODEL}\nLOCAL_EXTRACTION_ENABLED=true\nEOF\necho \"  Written to: $ENV_FILE\"\necho \"\"\n\n# --- 7. Validate ---\necho \"[7/7] Running validation...\"\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")\" && pwd)\"\nif [[ -f \"$SCRIPT_DIR/validate-model-endpoint.py\" ]]; then\n    uv run python \"$SCRIPT_DIR/validate-model-endpoint.py\"\nelse\n    echo \"  Validation script not found. Doing basic check...\"\n    if curl -s http://localhost:11434/api/tags | python3 -c \"import sys,json; models=json.load(sys.stdin).get('models',[]); print(f'  Found {len(models)} models: {[m[\\\"name\\\"] for m in models]}')\" 2>/dev/null; then\n        echo \"  Ollama is running with models available.\"\n    else\n        echo \"  WARNING: Could not verify Ollama models.\"\n    fi\nfi\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== Setup Complete ===\"\necho \"\"\necho \"To use local models:\"\necho \"  source .env.local\"\necho \"  # or add 'dotenv_path = \\\".env.local\\\"' to your .env\"\necho \"\"\necho \"To switch back to Gemini:\"\necho \"  unset MODEL_BACKEND OLLAMA_BASE_URL LOCAL_EXTRACTION_ENABLED\"\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/scripts/validate-model-endpoint.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Validate model endpoints: test Ollama, Gemini, and extraction endpoints.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nimport sys\n\nimport httpx\n\n\ndef check_ollama(base_url: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n    \"\"\"Check Ollama server health and available models.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        resp = httpx.get(f\"{base_url}/api/tags\", timeout=10.0)\n        resp.raise_for_status()\n        data = resp.json()\n        models = [m[\"name\"] for m in data.get(\"models\", [])]\n        return True, f\"OK — {len(models)} models: {models}\"\n    except Exception as e:\n        return False, f\"FAIL — {e}\"\n\n\ndef check_ollama_model(base_url: str, model: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n    \"\"\"Test a specific Ollama model with a simple prompt.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        resp = httpx.post(\n            f\"{base_url}/api/chat\",\n            json={\n                \"model\": model,\n                \"messages\": [{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": \"Say 'hello' and nothing else.\"}],\n                \"stream\": False,\n            },\n            timeout=120.0,\n        )\n        resp.raise_for_status()\n        content = resp.json()[\"message\"][\"content\"]\n        return True, f\"OK — response: {content[:80]}\"\n    except Exception as e:\n        return False, f\"FAIL — {e}\"\n\n\ndef check_gemini() -> tuple[bool, str]:\n    \"\"\"Check Gemini API key validity.\"\"\"\n    api_key = os.getenv(\"GOOGLE_API_KEY\")\n    if not api_key:\n        return False, \"SKIP — GOOGLE_API_KEY not set\"\n    try:\n        resp = httpx.get(\n            f\"https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key={api_key}\",\n            timeout=10.0,\n        )\n        if resp.status_code == 200:\n            models = resp.json().get(\"models\", [])\n            return True, f\"OK — {len(models)} models available\"\n        return False, f\"FAIL — HTTP {resp.status_code}\"\n    except Exception as e:\n        return False, f\"FAIL — {e}\"\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Run all health checks and print status table.\"\"\"\n    ollama_url = os.getenv(\"OLLAMA_BASE_URL\", \"http://localhost:11434\")\n    ollama_model = os.getenv(\"OLLAMA_MODEL\", \"gemma2:9b\")\n    extraction_model = os.getenv(\"OLLAMA_EXTRACTION_MODEL\", \"qwen2.5:14b\")\n\n    checks: list[tuple[str, bool, str]] = []\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"ElixirTrials Model Endpoint Validation\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print()\n\n    # Ollama server\n    ok, msg = check_ollama(ollama_url)\n    checks.append((\"Ollama Server\", ok, msg))\n    print(f\"  {'[PASS]' if ok else '[FAIL]'} Ollama Server: {msg}\")\n\n    if ok:\n        # Grounding model\n        ok2, msg2 = check_ollama_model(ollama_url, ollama_model)\n        checks.append((f\"Grounding ({ollama_model})\", ok2, msg2))\n        print(f\"  {'[PASS]' if ok2 else '[FAIL]'} Grounding ({ollama_model}): {msg2}\")\n\n        # Extraction model\n        ok3, msg3 = check_ollama_model(ollama_url, extraction_model)\n        checks.append((f\"Extraction ({extraction_model})\", ok3, msg3))\n        print(f\"  {'[PASS]' if ok3 else '[FAIL]'} Extraction ({extraction_model}): {msg3}\")\n\n    # Gemini API\n    ok4, msg4 = check_gemini()\n    checks.append((\"Gemini API\", ok4, msg4))\n    print(f\"  {'[PASS]' if ok4 else '[FAIL]'} Gemini API: {msg4}\")\n\n    print()\n    passed = sum(1 for _, ok, _ in checks if ok)\n    total = len(checks)\n    print(f\"Results: {passed}/{total} checks passed\")\n\n    if passed < total:\n        failed = [name for name, ok, _ in checks if not ok]\n        print(f\"Failed: {', '.join(failed)}\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n    else:\n        print(\"All checks passed!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

Setup\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Load environment variables and add workspace source paths.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"import sys\\n\",\n    \"from pathlib import Path\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Add workspace source paths\\n\",\n    \"ROOT = Path.cwd().parent if Path.cwd().name == \\\"notebooks\\\" else Path.cwd()\\n\",\n    \"for src_path in [\\n\",\n    \"    ROOT / \\\"services\\\" / \\\"protocol-processor-service\\\" / \\\"src\\\",\\n\",\n    \"    ROOT / \\\"services\\\" / \\\"api-service\\\" / \\\"src\\\",\\n\",\n    \"    ROOT / \\\"libs\\\" / \\\"inference\\\" / \\\"src\\\",\\n\",\n    \"    ROOT / \\\"libs\\\" / \\\"shared\\\" / \\\"src\\\",\\n\",\n    \"    ROOT / \\\"libs\\\" / \\\"events-py\\\" / \\\"src\\\",\\n\",\n    \"]:\\n\",\n    \"    if str(src_path) not in sys.path:\\n\",\n    \"        sys.path.insert(0, str(src_path))\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"# Load environment variables\\n\",\n    \"from dotenv import load_dotenv\\n\",\n    \"load_dotenv(ROOT / \\\".env\\\", override=False)\\n\",\n    \"load_dotenv(ROOT / \\\".env.local\\\", override=True)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"import os\\n\",\n    \"api_key = os.getenv(\\\"GOOGLE_API_KEY\\\")\\n\",\n    \"backend = os.getenv(\\\"MODEL_BACKEND\\\", \\\"gemini\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Backend: {backend}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"GOOGLE_API_KEY: {'set' if api_key else 'NOT SET'}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"OLLAMA_BASE_URL: {os.getenv('OLLAMA_BASE_URL', 'not set')}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"\\\\nWorkspace root: {ROOT}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## 2.      3→import os

    10→from api_service.storage import engine
    11→
    12→
    13→def get_db() -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    14→    """Provide a database session for dependency injection.
    15→
    16→    Yields:
    17→        Database session that automatically commits or rolls back.
    18→    """
    19→    with Session(engine) as session:
    20→        yield session
    21→
    22→
    23→def get_current_user(authorization: str | None = Header(default=None)) -> dict:
    24→    """Extract and validate JWT from Authorization header.
    25→
    26→    Expected format: Bearer <token>
    27→
    28→    Returns:
    29→        Dict with user claims: sub, email, name.
    30→
    31→    Raises:
    32→        HTTPException: 401 if token is missing, expired, or invalid.
    33→    """
    34→    if not authorization or not authorization.startswith("Bearer "):
    35→        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Missing or invalid auth header")
    36→
    37→    token = authorization[7:]
    38→    try:
    39→        secret = os.getenv("JWT_SECRET_KEY", "dev-secret-key-change-in-production")
    40→        payload = jwt.decode(token, secret, algorithms=["HS256"])
    41→        return payload
    42→    except jwt.ExpiredSignatureError:
    43→        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Token expired")
    44→    except jwt.InvalidTokenError:
    45→        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid token")

  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/api-service/src/api_service/criteria_table.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Criteria table API: paginated, filterable, sortable structured criteria view.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel\nfrom shared.models import Criteria, CriteriaBatch, Entity, Protocol\nfrom sqlmodel import Session, col, func, select\n\nfrom api_service.dependencies import get_db\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/criteria\", tags=[\"criteria-table\"])\n\n\nclass EntitySummary(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Compact entity representation for the criteria table.\"\"\"\n\n    id: str\n    entity_type: str\n    text: str\n    grounding_code: str | None = None\n    grounding_system: str | None = None\n    preferred_term: str | None = None\n    grounding_confidence: float | None = None\n\n\nclass StructuredCriterionRow(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Single row in the structured criteria table.\"\"\"\n\n    id: str\n    protocol_id: str | None = None\n    protocol_title: str | None = None\n    text: str\n    criteria_type: str\n    category: str | None = None\n    confidence: float\n    assertion_status: str | None = None\n    entities: list[EntitySummary] = []\n    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    structured_criterion: dict[str, Any] | None = None\n    review_status: str | None = None\n    page_number: int | None = None\n    source_section: str | None = None\n\n\nclass StructuredCriteriaResponse(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Paginated response for the criteria table.\"\"\"\n\n    items: list[StructuredCriterionRow]\n    total: int\n    page: int\n    page_size: int\n    pages: int\n\n\n@router.get(\"/structured\", response_model=StructuredCriteriaResponse)\ndef get_structured_criteria(\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),\n    page_size: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200),\n    criteria_type: str | None = Query(None),\n    category: str | None = Query(None),\n    protocol_id: str | None = Query(None),\n    min_confidence: float | None = Query(None, ge=0.0, le=1.0),\n    sort_by: str = Query(\"confidence\", regex=\"^(confidence|criteria_type|category|page_number|review_status)$\"),\n    sort_order: str = Query(\"desc\", regex=\"^(asc|desc)$\"),\n    has_grounding: bool | None = Query(None),\n) -> StructuredCriteriaResponse:\n    \"\"\"Paginated, filterable, sortable view of structured criteria across all protocols.\"\"\"\n\n    # Base query: join Criteria -> CriteriaBatch -> Protocol\n    query = (\n        select(Criteria, CriteriaBatch.protocol_id, Protocol.title)\n        .join(CriteriaBatch, col(Criteria.batch_id) == col(CriteriaBatch.id))\n        .join(Protocol, col(CriteriaBatch.protocol_id) == col(Protocol.id))\n        .where(col(CriteriaBatch.is_archived) == False)  # noqa: E712\n    )\n\n    # Filters\n    if criteria_type:\n        query = query.where(col(Criteria.criteria_type) == criteria_type)\n    if category:\n        query = query.where(col(Criteria.category) == category)\n    if protocol_id:\n        query = query.where(col(CriteriaBatch.protocol_id) == protocol_id)\n    if min_confidence is not None:\n        query = query.where(col(Criteria.confidence) >= min_confidence)\n\n    # Count total\n    count_query = select(func.count()).select_from(query.subquery())\n    total = db.exec(count_query).one()\n\n    # Sort\n    sort_col = getattr(Criteria, sort_by, Criteria.confidence)\n    if sort_order == \"desc\":\n        query = query.order_by(col(sort_col).desc())\n    else:\n        query = query.order_by(col(sort_col).asc())\n\n    # Paginate\n    offset = (page - 1) * page_size\n    query = query.offset(offset).limit(page_size)\n\n    results = db.exec(query).all()\n\n    # Build response rows\n    items: list[StructuredCriterionRow] = []\n    criteria_ids = []\n\n    for row in results:\n        criterion = row[0]\n        proto_id = row[1]\n        proto_title = row[2]\n        criteria_ids.append(criterion.id)\n\n        # Extract field_mappings from conditions JSON\n        field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n        if criterion.conditions and isinstance(criterion.conditions, dict):\n            fm = criterion.conditions.get(\"field_mappings\", [])\n            if isinstance(fm, list):\n                field_mappings = fm\n\n        items.append(\n            StructuredCriterionRow(\n                id=criterion.id,\n                protocol_id=proto_id,\n                protocol_title=proto_title,\n                text=criterion.text,\n                criteria_type=criterion.criteria_type,\n                category=criterion.category,\n                confidence=criterion.confidence,\n                assertion_status=criterion.assertion_status,\n                field_mappings=field_mappings,\n                structured_criterion=criterion.structured_criterion,\n                review_status=criterion.review_status,\n                page_number=criterion.page_number,\n                source_section=criterion.source_section,\n            )\n        )\n\n    # Batch-load entities for all criteria\n    if criteria_ids:\n        entities_query = select(Entity).where(col(Entity.criteria_id).in_(criteria_ids))\n        entities = db.exec(entities_query).all()\n\n        entity_map: dict[str, list[EntitySummary]] = {}\n        for ent in entities:\n            code = ent.snomed_code or ent.umls_cui or ent.rxnorm_code or ent.icd10_code or ent.loinc_code or ent.hpo_code\n            system = ent.grounding_system\n            summary = EntitySummary(\n                id=ent.id,\n                entity_type=ent.entity_type,\n                text=ent.text,\n                grounding_code=code,\n                grounding_system=system,\n                preferred_term=ent.preferred_term,\n                grounding_confidence=ent.grounding_confidence,\n            )\n            entity_map.setdefault(ent.criteria_id, []).append(summary)\n\n        for item in items:\n            item.entities = entity_map.get(item.id, [])\n\n    # Filter by grounding post-query (entity-level filter)\n    if has_grounding is not None:\n        if has_grounding:\n            items = [i for i in items if any(e.grounding_code for e in i.entities)]\n        else:\n            items = [i for i in items if not any(e.grounding_code for e in i.entities)]\n        total = len(items)\n\n    pages = max(1, (total + page_size - 1) // page_size)\n\n    return StructuredCriteriaResponse(\n        items=items,\n        total=total,\n        page=page,\n        page_size=page_size,\n        pages=pages,\n    )\n"
}

  "content": "import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';\n\nimport { fetchApi } from '../lib/fetchApi';\n\nexport interface EntitySummary {\n\tid: string;\n\tentity_type: string;\n\ttext: string;\n\tgrounding_code: string | null;\n\tgrounding_system: string | null;\n\tpreferred_term: string | null;\n\tgrounding_confidence: number | null;\n}\n\nexport interface StructuredCriterionRow {\n\tid: string;\n\tprotocol_id: string | null;\n\tprotocol_title: string | null;\n\ttext: string;\n\tcriteria_type: string;\n\tcategory: string | null;\n\tconfidence: number;\n\tassertion_status: string | null;\n\tentities: EntitySummary[];\n\tfield_mappings: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;\n\tstructured_criterion: Record<string, unknown> | null;\n\treview_status: string | null;\n\tpage_number: number | null;\n\tsource_section: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface StructuredCriteriaResponse {\n\titems: StructuredCriterionRow[];\n\ttotal: number;\n\tpage: number;\n\tpage_size: number;\n\tpages: number;\n}\n\nexport interface CriteriaFilters {\n\tpage: number;\n\tpageSize: number;\n\tcriteriaType?: string;\n\tcategory?: string;\n\tprotocolId?: string;\n\tminConfidence?: number;\n\tsortBy?: string;\n\tsortOrder?: 'asc' | 'desc';\n\thasGrounding?: boolean;\n}\n\nexport function useCriteriaSpreadsheet(filters: CriteriaFilters) {\n\tconst params = new URLSearchParams({\n\t\tpage: String(filters.page),\n\t\tpage_size: String(filters.pageSize),\n\t});\n\n\tif (filters.criteriaType) params.set('criteria_type', filters.criteriaType);\n\tif (filters.category) params.set('category', filters.category);\n\tif (filters.protocolId) params.set('protocol_id', filters.protocolId);\n\tif (filters.minConfidence !== undefined) params.set('min_confidence', String(filters.minConfidence));\n\tif (filters.sortBy) params.set('sort_by', filters.sortBy);\n\tif (filters.sortOrder) params.set('sort_order', filters.sortOrder);\n\tif (filters.hasGrounding !== undefined) params.set('has_grounding', String(filters.hasGrounding));\n\n\treturn useQuery({\n\t\tqueryKey: ['criteria-structured', filters],\n\t\tqueryFn: () => fetchApi<StructuredCriteriaResponse>(`/criteria/structured?${params.toString()}`),\n\t\tstaleTime: 30_000,\n\t});\n}\n"
}

'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300'\n\t\t\t: value >= 0.5\n\t\t\t\t? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border-amber-300'\n\t\t\t\t: 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 border-red-300';\n\n\tconst barColor = value >= 0.7 ? 'bg-emerald-500' : value >= 0.5 ? 'bg-amber-500' : 'bg-red-500';\n\n\treturn (\n\t\t<div className={cn('inline-flex items-center gap-1.5', className)}>\n\t\t\t<span\n\t\t\t\tclassName={cn(\n\t\t\t\t\t'inline-flex items-center rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold tabular-nums',\n\t\t\t\t\tcolorClass,\n\t\t\t\t)}\n\t\t\t>\n\t\t\t\t{pct}%\n\t\t\t</span>\n\t\t\t<div className=\"w-12 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-muted overflow-hidden\">\n\t\t\t\t<div\n\t\t\t\t\tclassName={cn('h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500 ease-out', barColor)}\n\t\t\t\t\tstyle={{ width: `${pct}%` }}\n\t\t\t\t/>\n\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t</div>\n\t);\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/apps/hitl-ui/src/components/criteria-table/ExpressionTreeCell.tsx",
  "content": "interface ExpressionNode {\n\ttype: string;\n\tentity?: string;\n\trelation?: string;\n\tvalue?: string;\n\tunit?: string;\n\tchildren?: ExpressionNode[];\n}\n\ninterface ExpressionTreeCellProps {\n\ttree: Record<string, unknown> | null;\n\texpanded?: boolean;\n}\n\nfunction NodeBadge({ type }: { type: string }) {\n\tconst colors: Record<string, string> = {\n\t\tAND: 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 border-purple-300',\n\t\tOR: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 border-blue-300',\n\t\tNOT: 'bg-red-100 text-red-700 border-red-300',\n\t\tATOMIC: 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 border-gray-300',\n\t};\n\n\treturn (\n\t\t<span\n\t\t\tclassName={`inline-flex items-center rounded border px-1 py-0 text-[10px] font-bold ${colors[type] || colors.ATOMIC}`}\n\t\t>\n\t\t\t{type}\n\t\t</span>\n\t);\n}\n\nfunction TreeNode({ node, depth = 0 }: { node: ExpressionNode; depth?: number }) {\n\tconst indent = depth * 16;\n\n\tif (node.type === 'ATOMIC') {\n\t\treturn (\n\t\t\t<div className=\"flex items-center gap-1 text-xs\" style={{ paddingLeft: indent }}>\n\t\t\t\t<span className=\"text-muted-foreground\">&#x25cf;</span>\n\t\t\t\t<span className=\"font-medium\">{node.entity || '?'}</span>\n\t\t\t\t{node.relation && <span className=\"text-blue-600 font-mono\">{node.relation}</span>}\n\t\t\t\t{node.value && <span>{node.value}</span>}\n\t\t\t\t{node.unit && <span className=\"text-muted-foreground\">{node.unit}</span>}\n\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t);\n\t}\n\n\treturn (\n\t\t<div style={{ paddingLeft: indent }}>\n\t\t\t<NodeBadge type={node.type} />\n\t\t\t{node.children?.map((child, i) => (\n\t\t\t\t// biome-ignore lint/suspicious/noArrayIndexKey: tree nodes have no stable id\n\t\t\t\t<TreeNode key={i} node={child as ExpressionNode} depth={depth + 1} />\n\t\t\t))}\n\t\t</div>\n\t);\n}\n\nexport function ExpressionTreeCell({ tree, expanded = false }: ExpressionTreeCellProps) {\n\tif (!tree) return <span className=\"text-muted-foreground text-xs italic\">—</span>;\n\n\tconst root = (tree.root || tree) as ExpressionNode;\n\n\tif (!expanded && root.type !== 'ATOMIC') {\n\t\tconst childCount = root.children?.length || 0;\n\t\treturn (\n\t\t\t<div className=\"flex items-center gap-1\">\n\t\t\t\t<NodeBadge type={root.type} />\n\t\t\t\t<span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">({childCount} children)</span>\n\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t);\n\t}\n\n\treturn (\n\t\t<div className=\"space-y-0.5\">\n\t\t\t<TreeNode node={root} />\n\t\t</div>\n\t);\n}\n"
}

  "content": "interface RawMapping {\n\tentity: string;\n\tentity_code?: string;\n\tentity_system?: string;\n\tomop_concept_id?: string;\n\trelation: string;\n\tvalue: unknown;\n\tunit?: string;\n}\n\ninterface FieldMappingCellProps {\n\tmappings: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;\n}\n\nfunction formatValue(value: unknown, unit?: string): string {\n\tif (value === null || value === undefined) return '';\n\tif (typeof value === 'string' || typeof value === 'number') {\n\t\treturn unit ? `${value} ${unit}` : String(value);\n\t}\n\tif (typeof value === 'object') {\n\t\tconst v = value as Record<string, unknown>;\n\t\tif (v.type === 'range') return `${v.min}–${v.max}${v.unit ? ` ${v.unit}` : ''}`;\n\t\tif (v.type === 'temporal') return `${v.duration} ${v.unit}`;\n\t\tif (v.type === 'standard') return `${v.value}${v.unit ? ` ${v.unit}` : ''}`;\n\t}\n\treturn '';\n}\n\nfunction MappingPill({ mapping }: { mapping: RawMapping }) {\n\tconst valueText = formatValue(mapping.value, mapping.unit);\n\n\treturn (\n\t\t<div className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border bg-blue-50/60 border-blue-200 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px]\">\n\t\t\t<span className=\"font-semibold text-blue-900\">{mapping.entity}</span>\n\t\t\t{mapping.entity_code && (\n\t\t\t\t<span className=\"rounded-full bg-green-50 border border-green-200 px-1 py-0 text-[9px] text-green-700\">\n\t\t\t\t\t{mapping.entity_system?.toUpperCase()}: {mapping.entity_code}\n\t\t\t\t</span>\n\t\t\t)}\n\t\t\t{mapping.relation && <span className=\"text-blue-600 font-mono\">{mapping.relation}</span>}\n\t\t\t{valueText && <span className=\"text-blue-800\">{valueText}</span>}\n\t\t</div>\n\t);\n}\n\nexport function FieldMappingCell({ mappings }: FieldMappingCellProps) {\n\tif (!mappings || mappings.length === 0) {\n\t\treturn <span className=\"text-muted-foreground text-xs italic\">—</span>;\n\t}\n\n\treturn (\n\t\t<div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-1\">\n\t\t\t{mappings.slice(0, 3).map((m, i) => (\n\t\t\t\t// biome-ignore lint/suspicious/noArrayIndexKey: mappings have no stable id\n\t\t\t\t<MappingPill key={i} mapping={m as unknown as RawMapping} />\n\t\t\t))}\n\t\t\t{mappings.length > 3 && (\n\t\t\t\t<span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">+{mappings.length - 3} more</span>\n\t\t\t)}\n\t\t</div>\n\t);\n}\n"
}
