duration-200 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-left-1\">\n\t\t\t<span className=\"text-blue-500\">{label}:</span>\n\t\t\t<span>{value}</span>\n\t\t\t<button\n\t\t\t\ttype=\"button\"\n\t\t\t\tonClick={onRemove}\n\t\t\t\tclassName=\"ml-0.5 rounded-full p-0.5 hover:bg-blue-200 transition-colors\"\n\t\t\t>\n\t\t\t\t<X className=\"h-3 w-3\" />\n\t\t\t</button>\n\t\t</span>\n\t);\n}\n\ninterface FilterBarProps {\n\tcriteriaType: string;\n\tsetCriteriaType: (v: string) => void;\n\tcategory: string;\n\tsetCategory: (v: string) => void;\n\tminConfidence: string;\n\tsetMinConfidence: (v: string) => void;\n}\n\nconst CRITERIA_TYPES = ['inclusion', 'exclusion'];\nconst CATEGORIES = ['medical_history', 'lab_values', 'medications', 'procedures', 'demographics', 'other'];\n\nexport function FilterBar({\n\tcriteriaType,\n\tsetCriteriaType,\n\tcategory,\n\tsetCategory,\n\tminConfidence,\n\tsetMinConfidence,\n}: FilterBarProps) {\n\treturn (\n\t\t<div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3\">\n\t\t\t<select\n\t\t\t\tvalue={criteriaType}\n\t\t\t\tonChange={(e) => setCriteriaType(e.target.value)}\n\t\t\t\tclassName=\"rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-3 py-1.5 text-sm ring-offset-background focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring\"\n\t\t\t>\n\t\t\t\t<option value=\"\">All types</option>\n\t\t\t\t{CRITERIA_TYPES.map((t) => (\n\t\t\t\t\t<option key={t} value={t}>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t{t.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + t.slice(1)}\n\t\t\t\t\t</option>\n\t\t\t\t))}\n\t\t\t</select>\n\n\t\t\t<select\n\t\t\t\tvalue={category}\n\t\t\t\tonChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)}\n\t\t\t\tclassName=\"rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-3 py-1.5 text-sm ring-offset-background focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring\"\n\t\t\t>\n\t\t\t\t<option value=\"\">All categories</option>\n\t\t\t\t{CATEGORIES.map((c) => (\n\t\t\t\t\t<option key={c} value={c}>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t{c.replace(/_/g, ' ')}\n\t\t\t\t\t</option>\n\t\t\t\t))}\n\t\t\t</select>\n\n\t\t\t<div className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5\">\n\t\t\t\t<label htmlFor=\"min-confidence\" className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">\n\t\t\t\t\tMin confidence:\n\t\t\t\t</label>\n\t\t\t\t<input\n\t\t\t\t\tid=\"min-confidence\"\n\t\t\t\t\ttype=\"number\"\n\t\t\t\t\tmin=\"0\"\n\t\t\t\t\tmax=\"1\"\n\t\t\t\t\tstep=\"0.1\"\n\t\t\t\t\tvalue={minConfidence}\n\t\t\t\t\tonChange={(e) => setMinConfidence(e.target.value)}\n\t\t\t\t\tclassName=\"w-16 rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-2 py-1.5 text-sm ring-offset-background focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring\"\n\t\t\t\t\tplaceholder=\"0.0\"\n\t\t\t\t/>\n\t\t\t</div>\n\n\t\t\t{/* Active filter chips */}\n\t\t\t<div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-1.5\">\n\t\t\t\t{criteriaType && (\n\t\t\t\t\t<FilterChip label=\"Type\" value={criteriaType} onRemove={() => setCriteriaType('')} />\n\t\t\t\t)}\n\t\t\t\t{category && (\n\t\t\t\t\t<FilterChip\n\t\t\t\t\t\tlabel=\"Category\"\n\t\t\t\t\t\tvalue={category.replace(/_/g, ' ')}\n\t\t\t\t\t\tonRemove={() => setCategory('')}\n\t\t\t\t\t/>\n\t\t\t\t)}\n\t\t\t\t{minConfidence && (\n\t\t\t\t\t<FilterChip\n\t\t\t\t\t\tlabel=\"Min conf\"\n\t\t\t\t\t\tvalue={minConfidence}\n\t\t\t\t\t\tonRemove={() => { category: string | null }) {\n\tif (!category) return <span className=\"text-muted-foreground text-xs\">—</span>;\n\n\tconst colors: Record<string, string> = {\n\t\tmedical_history: 'bg-violet-100 colors.other\n\t\t\t}`}\n\t\t>\n\t\t\t{category.replace(/_/g, ' ')}\n\t\t</span>\n\t);\n}\n\nfunction ReviewBadge({ status }: { status: string | null }) {\n\tif (!status) return <span 'bg-red-100 text-red-800 border-red-300',\n\t\tmodified: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border-amber-300',\n\t\tpending: 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 border-gray-300',\n\t};\n\n\treturn colors.pending\n\t\t\t}`}\n\t\t>\n\t\t\t{status}\n\t\t</span>\n\t);\n}\n\nfunction mapSystem(system: string | null): TerminologySystem | null {\n\tif (!system) return null;\n\tconst lower = system.toLowerCase();\n\tconst map: Record<string, TerminologySystem> = {\n\t\trxnorm: 'rxnorm',\n\t\t'icd-10': 'icd10',\n\t\ticd10: 'icd10',\n\t\tsnomed: 'snomed',\n\t\tloinc: 'loinc',\n\t\thpo: (\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">+{entities.length - 3}</span>\n\t\t\t\t\t)}\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t);\n\t\t},\n\t},\n\t{\n\t\taccessorKey: > AGENT

All three features implemented. Here's a summary:

## Summary

### Feature 3: Unified Inference Gateway (implemented first)
**New files (8):**
- `libs/inference/src/inference/adapters/base.py` — `BaseAdapter` ABC
- `libs/inference/src/inference/adapters/gemini_adapter.py` — Gemini File API adapter
- `libs/inference/src/inference/adapters/vertex_adapter.py` — Vertex AI adapter
- `libs/inference/src/inference/adapters/ollama_adapter.py` — Local Ollama adapter with PDF text extraction
- `libs/inference/src/inference/file_store.py` — Local file store
- `libs/inference/src/inference/router.py` — `ModelRouter` maps roles to adapters
- `libs/inference/src/inference/gateway.py` — `InferenceGateway` unified entry point
- `scripts/setup-local-models.sh` — Interactive setup script (detect HW, install Ollama, pull models)
- `scripts/validate-model-endpoint.py` — Health check validation

**Modified files:**
- `libs/inference/src/inference/config.py` — Added `ollama_base_url`, `ollama_model`, `ollama_extraction_model` fields; accepts "ollama" backend
- `libs/inference/src/inference/model_garden.py` — Added `_create_ollama_model_loader()` via ChatOllama
- `gemini_extractor.py` — Gateway integration when `LOCAL_EXTRACTION_ENABLED=true`
- `gemini_utils.py` — Ollama fallback when `GOOGLE_API_KEY` not set but `OLLAMA_BASE_URL` configured
- `pyproject.toml` — Added `langchain-community` dependency

### Feature 1: Jupyter Notebook Demo
**New files (2):**
- `notebooks/criteria_extraction_demo.ipynb` — 7-section demo (Setup, PDF Ingestion, Extraction, Rich Display, Grounding, Expression Trees, Field Mappings)
- `notebooks/sample_data/README.md` — Instructions for sample PDFs
- `pyproject.toml` — Added `matplotlib`, `pandas` to dev deps

### Feature 2: Frontend Criteria Spreadsheet
**Backend (1 new file):**
- `services/api-service/src/api_service/criteria_table.py` — `GET /criteria/structured` paginated, filterable, sortable endpoint

**Frontend (8 new files):**
- `CriteriaSpreadsheet.tsx` — Main screen with filter bar + table + stats
- `useCriteriaSpreadsheet.ts` — React Query hook
- `CriteriaTable.tsx` — TanStack Table wrapper with expandable rows, sticky header, zebra striping
- `columns.tsx` — 11 column definitions with rich cell renderers
- `ExpressionTreeCell.tsx` — Inline recursive AND/OR/NOT tree
- `FieldMappingCell.tsx` — E|R|V|U compact pill display
- `ConfidenceBadge.tsx` — Color-gradient confidence bar
- `filters.tsx` — Filter chips with animated add/remove

**Modified:** `App.tsx` (route + nav), `main.py` (router registration), `package.json` (`@tanstack/react-table`)

### Verification
- **371 tests pass**, 0 warnings
- Frontend builds cleanly (TypeScript + Biome + Vite)
- All Python imports verified

```
     1→"""Criteria table API: paginated, filterable, sortable structured criteria view."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query
     9→from pydantic import BaseModel
    10→from shared.models import Criteria, CriteriaBatch, Entity, Protocol
    11→from sqlmodel import Session, col, func, select
    12→
    13→from api_service.dependencies import get_db
    14→
    15→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16→
    17→router = APIRouter(prefix="/criteria", tags=["criteria-table"])
    18→
    19→
    20→class EntitySummary(BaseModel):
    21→    """Compact entity representation for the criteria table."""
    22→
    23→    id: str
    24→    entity_type: str
    25→    text: str
    26→    grounding_code: str | None = None
    27→    grounding_system: str | None = None
    28→    preferred_term: str | None = None
    29→    grounding_confidence: float | None = None
    30→
    31→
    32→class StructuredCriterionRow(BaseModel):
    33→    """Single row in the structured criteria table."""
    34→
    35→    id: str
    36→    protocol_id: str | None = None
    37→    protocol_title: str | None = None
    38→    text: str
    39→    criteria_type: str
    40→    category: str | None = None
    41→    confidence: float
    42→    assertion_status: str | None = None
    43→    entities: list[EntitySummary] = []
    44→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    45→    structured_criterion: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    46→    review_status: str | None = None
    47→    page_number: int | None = None
    48→    source_section: str | None = None
    49→
    50→
    51→class StructuredCriteriaResponse(BaseModel):
    52→    """Paginated response for the criteria table."""
    53→
    54→    items: list[StructuredCriterionRow]
    55→    total: int
    56→    page: int
    57→    page_size: int
    58→    pages: int
    59→
    60→
    61→@router.get("/structured", response_model=StructuredCriteriaResponse)
    62→def get_structured_criteria(
    63→    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    64→    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    65→    page_size: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200),
    66→    criteria_type: str | None = Query(None),
    67→    category: str | None = Query(None),
    68→    protocol_id: str | None = Query(None),
    69→    min_confidence: float | None = Query(None, ge=0.0, le=1.0),
    70→    sort_by: str = Query("confidence", pattern="^(confidence|criteria_type|category|page_number|review_status)$"),
    71→    sort_order: str = Query("desc", pattern="^(asc|desc)$"),
    72→    has_grounding: bool | None = Query(None),
    73→) -> StructuredCriteriaResponse:
    74→    """Paginated, filterable, sortable view of structured criteria across all protocols."""
    75→    # Base query: join Criteria -> CriteriaBatch -> Protocol
    76→    query = (
    77→        select(Criteria, CriteriaBatch.protocol_id, Protocol.title)
    78→        .join(CriteriaBatch, col(Criteria.batch_id) == col(CriteriaBatch.id))
    79→        .join(Protocol, col(CriteriaBatch.protocol_id) == col(Protocol.id))
    80→        .where(col(CriteriaBatch.is_archived) == False)  # noqa: E712
    81→    )
    82→
    83→    # Filters
    84→    if criteria_type:
    85→        query = query.where(col(Criteria.criteria_type) == criteria_type)
    86→    if category:
    87→        query = query.where(col(Criteria.category) == category)
    88→    if protocol_id:
    89→        query = query.where(col(CriteriaBatch.protocol_id) == protocol_id)
    90→    if min_confidence is not None:
    91→        query = query.where(col(Criteria.confidence) >= min_confidence)
    92→
    93→    # Count total
    94→    count_query = select(func.count()).select_from(query.subquery())
    95→    total = db.exec(count_query).one()
    96→
    97→    # Sort
    98→    sort_col = getattr(Criteria, sort_by, Criteria.confidence)
    99→    if sort_order == "desc":
   100→        query = query.order_by(col(sort_col).desc())
   101→    else:
   102→        query = query.order_by(col(sort_col).asc())
   103→
   104→    # Paginate
   105→    offset = (page - 1) * page_size
   106→    query = query.offset(offset).limit(page_size)
   107→
   108→    results = db.exec(query).all()
   109→
   110→    # Build response rows
   111→    items: list[StructuredCriterionRow] = []
   112→    criteria_ids = []
   113→
   114→    for row in results:
   115→        criterion = row[0]
   116→        proto_id = row[1]
   117→        proto_title = row[2]
   118→        criteria_ids.append(criterion.id)
   119→
   120→        # Extract field_mappings from conditions JSON
   121→        field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   122→        if criterion.conditions and isinstance(criterion.conditions, dict):
   123→            fm = criterion.conditions.get("field_mappings", [])
   124→            if isinstance(fm, list):
   125→                field_mappings = fm
   126→
   127→        items.append(
   128→            StructuredCriterionRow(
   129→                id=criterion.id,
   130→                protocol_id=proto_id,
   131→                protocol_title=proto_title,
   132→                text=criterion.text,
   133→                criteria_type=criterion.criteria_type,
   134→                category=criterion.category,
   135→                confidence=criterion.confidence,
   136→                assertion_status=criterion.assertion_status,
   137→                field_mappings=field_mappings,
   138→                structured_criterion=criterion.structured_criterion,
   139→                review_status=criterion.review_status,
   140→                page_number=criterion.page_number,
   141→                source_section=criterion.source_section,
   142→            )
   143→        )
   144→
   145→    # Batch-load entities for all criteria
   146→    if criteria_ids:
   147→        entities_query = select(Entity).where(col(Entity.criteria_id).in_(criteria_ids))
   148→        entities = db.exec(entities_query).all()
   149→
   150→        entity_map: dict[str, list[EntitySummary]] = {}
   151→        for ent in entities:
   152→            code = ent.snomed_code or ent.umls_cui or ent.rxnorm_code or ent.icd10_code or ent.loinc_code or ent.hpo_code
   153→            system = ent.grounding_system
   154→            summary = EntitySummary(
   155→                id=ent.id,
   156→                entity_type=ent.entity_type,
   157→                text=ent.text,
   158→                grounding_code=code,
   159→                grounding_system=system,
   160→                preferred_term=ent.preferred_term,
   161→                grounding_confidence=ent.grounding_confidence,
   162→            )
   163→            entity_map.setdefault(ent.criteria_id, []).append(summary)
   164→
   165→        for item in items:
   166→            item.entities = entity_map.get(item.id, [])
   167→
   168→    # Filter by grounding post-query (entity-level filter)
   169→    if has_grounding is not None:
   170→        if has_grounding:
   171→            items = [i for i in items if any(e.grounding_code for e in i.entities)]
   172→        else:
   173→            items = [i for i in items if not any(e.grounding_code for e in i.entities)]
   174→        total = len(items)
   175→
   176→    pages = max(1, (total + page_size - 1) // page_size)
   177→
   178→    return StructuredCriteriaResponse(
   179→        items=items,
   180→        total=total,
   181→        page=page,
   182→        page_size=page_size,
   183→        pages=pages,
   184→    )
   185→
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/api-service/src/api_service/criteria_table.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Criteria table API: paginated, filterable, sortable structured criteria view.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query\nfrom pydantic import BaseModel\nfrom shared.models import Criteria, CriteriaBatch, Entity, Protocol\nfrom sqlmodel import Session, col, func, select\n\nfrom api_service.dependencies import get_db\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/criteria\", tags=[\"criteria-table\"])\n\n_SORT_BY_PATTERN = (\n    \"^(confidence|criteria_type|category\"\n    \"|page_number|review_status)$\"\n)\n\n\nclass EntitySummary(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Compact entity representation for the criteria table.\"\"\"\n\n    id: str\n    entity_type: str\n    text: str\n    grounding_code: str | None = None\n    grounding_system: str | None = None\n    preferred_term: str | None = None\n    grounding_confidence: float | None = None\n\n\nclass StructuredCriterionRow(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Single row in the structured criteria table.\"\"\"\n\n    id: str\n    protocol_id: str | None = None\n    protocol_title: str | None = None\n    text: str\n    criteria_type: str\n    category: str | None = None\n    confidence: float\n    assertion_status: str | None = None\n    entities: list[EntitySummary] = []\n    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    structured_criterion: dict[str, Any] | None = None\n    review_status: str | None = None\n    page_number: int | None = None\n    source_section: str | None = None\n\n\nclass StructuredCriteriaResponse(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"Paginated response for the criteria table.\"\"\"\n\n    items: list[StructuredCriterionRow]\n    total: int\n    page: int\n    page_size: int\n    pages: int\n\n\ndef _pick_grounding_code(ent: Entity) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Pick the first non-null grounding code from an entity.\"\"\"\n    return (\n        ent.snomed_code\n        or ent.umls_cui\n        or ent.rxnorm_code\n        or ent.icd10_code\n        or ent.loinc_code\n        or ent.hpo_code\n    )\n\n\ndef _build_base_query():  # noqa: ANN202\n    \"\"\"Build base SELECT joining Criteria -> Batch -> Protocol.\"\"\"\n    return (\n        select(Criteria, CriteriaBatch.protocol_id, Protocol.title)\n        .join(\n            CriteriaBatch,\n            col(Criteria.batch_id) == col(CriteriaBatch.id),\n        )\n        .join(\n            Protocol,\n            col(CriteriaBatch.protocol_id) == col(Protocol.id),\n        )\n        .where(col(CriteriaBatch.is_archived) == False)  # noqa: E712\n    )\n\n\ndef _apply_filters(query, *, criteria_type, category, protocol_id, min_confidence):  # noqa: ANN001, ANN202\n    \"\"\"Apply optional WHERE clauses to the query.\"\"\"\n    if criteria_type:\n        query = query.where(\n            col(Criteria.criteria_type) == criteria_type\n        )\n    if category:\n        query = query.where(col(Criteria.category) == category)\n    if protocol_id:\n        query = query.where(\n            col(CriteriaBatch.protocol_id) == protocol_id\n        )\n    if min_confidence is not None:\n        query = query.where(\n            col(Criteria.confidence) >= min_confidence\n        )\n    return query\n\n\ndef _load_entities(\n    db: Session,\n    criteria_ids: list[str],\n) -> dict[str, list[EntitySummary]]:\n    \"\"\"Batch-load entities and group by criteria_id.\"\"\"\n    entities_q = select(Entity).where(\n        col(Entity.criteria_id).in_(criteria_ids)\n    )\n    entities = db.exec(entities_q).all()\n\n    entity_map: dict[str, list[EntitySummary]] = {}\n    for ent in entities:\n        summary = EntitySummary(\n            id=ent.id,\n            entity_type=ent.entity_type,\n            text=ent.text,\n            grounding_code=_pick_grounding_code(ent),\n            grounding_system=ent.grounding_system,\n            preferred_term=ent.preferred_term,\n            grounding_confidence=ent.grounding_confidence,\n        )\n        entity_map.setdefault(ent.criteria_id, []).append(summary)\n    return entity_map\n\n\n@router.get(\n    \"/structured\",\n    response_model=StructuredCriteriaResponse,\n)\ndef get_structured_criteria(\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),\n    page_size: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200),\n    criteria_type: str | None = Query(None),\n    category: str | None = Query(None),\n    protocol_id: str | None = Query(None),\n    min_confidence: float | None = Query(None, ge=0.0, le=1.0),\n    sort_by: str = Query(\"confidence\", pattern=_SORT_BY_PATTERN),\n    sort_order: str = Query(\"desc\", pattern=\"^(asc|desc)$\"),\n    has_grounding: bool | None = Query(None),\n) -> StructuredCriteriaResponse:\n    \"\"\"Paginated, filterable, sortable structured criteria.\"\"\"\n    query = _build_base_query()\n    query = _apply_filters(\n        query,\n        criteria_type=criteria_type,\n        category=category,\n        protocol_id=protocol_id,\n        min_confidence=min_confidence,\n    )\n\n    # Count total\n    count_q = select(func.count()).select_from(query.subquery())\n    total = db.exec(count_q).one()\n\n    # Sort\n    sort_col = getattr(Criteria, sort_by, Criteria.confidence)\n    if sort_order == \"desc\":\n        query = query.order_by(col(sort_col).desc())\n    else:\n        query = query.order_by(col(sort_col).asc())\n\n    # Paginate\n    offset = (page - 1) * page_size\n    query = query.offset(offset).limit(page_size)\n    results = db.exec(query).all()\n\n    # Build response rows\n    items: list[StructuredCriterionRow] = []\n    criteria_ids: list[str] = []\n\n    for row in results:\n        criterion, proto_id, proto_title = row[0], row[1], row[2]\n        criteria_ids.append(criterion.id)\n\n        field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n        conds = criterion.conditions\n        if conds and isinstance(conds, dict):\n            fm = conds.get(\"field_mappings\", [])\n            if isinstance(fm, list):\n                field_mappings = fm\n\n        items.append(\n            StructuredCriterionRow(\n                id=criterion.id,\n                protocol_id=proto_id,\n                protocol_title=proto_title,\n                text=criterion.text,\n                criteria_type=criterion.criteria_type,\n                category=criterion.category,\n                confidence=criterion.confidence,\n                assertion_status=criterion.assertion_status,\n                field_mappings=field_mappings,\n                structured_criterion=criterion.structured_criterion,\n                review_status=criterion.review_status,\n                page_number=criterion.page_number,\n                source_section=criterion.source_section,\n            )\n        )\n\n    # Batch-load entities\n    if criteria_ids:\n        entity_map = _load_entities(db, criteria_ids)\n        for item in items:\n            item.entities = entity_map.get(item.id, [])\n\n    # Filter by grounding post-query\n    if has_grounding is not None:\n        if has_grounding:\n            items = [\n                i for i in items\n                if any(e.grounding_code for e in i.entities)\n            ]\n        else:\n            items = [\n                i for i in items\n                if not any(e.grounding_code for e in i.entities)\n            ]\n        total = len(items)\n\n    pages = max(1, (total + page_size - 1) // page_size)\n\n    return StructuredCriteriaResponse(\n        items=items,\n        total=total,\n        page=page,\n        page_size=page_size,\n        pages=pages,\n    )\n"
}
```
